   387	## Phase 2 — impact resolution
   388	Re-derive `CONFIG_SHA` from `EVIDENCE` as specified above.
   389	Build a concise `changeSummary` (per changed file: path + 1-line nature of change from `git diff --stat`/`git show`); it depends only on the Phase 1 `changed` list. When `CM_AVAILABLE` is true, derive this `changeSummary` with context-mode instead of reading raw diffs into context: run the `git diff`/`git show` through `ctx_execute` (or `ctx_batch_execute`) in the sandbox and return only the compact per-file summary — the raw diff stays out of context, so every downstream subagent prompt is smaller too. When `CM_AVAILABLE` is false, build it from `git diff --stat`/`git show` as usual.
   390	`RUN_DIR` is the run-scoped directory returned by `open-run.py`; never reset it to the old flat
   391	`.claude/state/docaudit-run` path and never create it yourself. Capture impact output there:
   392	`printf '%s\n' "${changed[@]}" | python3 "$SD/scripts/resolve-impact.py" --config "$CFG" --expect-config-sha "$CONFIG_SHA" --repo-root "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR" --changed - --mode "$MODE" --history "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/state/docaudit-history.json" > "$RUN_DIR/impact.json"`.
   393	Parse `$RUN_DIR/impact.json` for `{impacted[], mapGapCandidates[], ssotRecheck[], warnings[], truncated, counts{changed,impacted,mapped,heuristicOnly,regression,docCorpus,heuristicSaturation,candidatesBeforeCap}}`. If `truncated` is true, record the dropped count (the script also prints it to stderr) explicitly in the Phase 5 report — never silently discard it. If `warnings` is non-empty (e.g. an `ssotSources` entry with a URL `liveSource`, which is never fetched or verified), carry them to the Phase-5 warning lines — never silently discard them.
   394	
   395	When `DOC_GRAPH_AVAILABLE` or `SEMANTIC_SEARCH_AVAILABLE` is true, supplement `impact.json` with
   396	graphify/CocoIndex candidates before classification and dispatch (either or both — each is an independent,
   397	optional source). Bind its inputs first:
   398	`MAX_IMPACTED_DOCS="$(python3 "$SD/scripts/sealed_config.py" --config "$CFG" --expect-sha "$CONFIG_SHA" --get maxImpactedDocs --default 200)"`.
   399	`DOC_GLOBS_JSON="$(python3 "$SD/scripts/sealed_config.py" --config "$CFG" --expect-sha "$CONFIG_SHA" --get docGlobs --default '[]')"`; comma-join this JSON array without reading `CFG` as `DOC_GLOBS="$(python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(",".join(json.loads(sys.argv[1])))' "$DOC_GLOBS_JSON")"`.
   400	`SEMANTIC_MIN_SCORE="$(python3 "$SD/scripts/sealed_config.py" --config "$CFG" --expect-sha "$CONFIG_SHA" --get semanticSearch.minScore --default 0.4)"`.
   401	Then invoke the supplement as an
   402	optional source): `python3 "$SD/scripts/impact-supplement.py" --impact-json "$RUN_DIR/impact.json"
   403	--changed - --change-summary "$changeSummary" --repo-root "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR" --config "$CFG" --expect-config-sha "$CONFIG_SHA"
   404	--max-impacted-docs "$MAX_IMPACTED_DOCS" --doc-globs "$DOC_GLOBS"
   405	[--graphify-bin "$DOC_GRAPH_BIN"] [--cocoindex-bin "$SEMANTIC_SEARCH_BIN" --min-score
   406	"$SEMANTIC_MIN_SCORE"]`, piping the Phase-1 `changed` list to stdin — include
   407	`--graphify-bin` only when `DOC_GRAPH_AVAILABLE` is true, and `--cocoindex-bin`/`--min-score` only
   408	when `SEMANTIC_SEARCH_AVAILABLE` is true. It rewrites `$RUN_DIR/impact.json` in place: re-parse it
   409	afterward (it may now carry updated `counts.graphifyOnly`/`counts.semanticOnly`/`truncated`/
   410	`warnings[]`) before proceeding. `resolve-impact.py`'s own `mapped`/`heuristic` result is never
   411	displaced — new candidates only ever fill the residual slots left under `maxImpactedDocs`, strictly
   412	`mapped` ≥ `regression` ≥ `heuristic` ≥ `graphify` ≥ `semantic` (Issue #8 anti-regression). When both
   413	`DOC_GRAPH_AVAILABLE` and `SEMANTIC_SEARCH_AVAILABLE` are false, skip this step entirely.
   414	
   415	Classify the run deterministically:
   416	`python3 "$SD/scripts/classify-run.py" --repo-root "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR" --config "$CFG" --expect-config-sha "$CONFIG_SHA" --impact-json "$RUN_DIR/impact.json" --baseline-sha "$EFFECTIVE_BASELINE_SHA" --mode "$MODE" --last-run "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/state/docaudit-last-run.json"`.
   417	Bind `RUN_CLASS` from `runClass` (`light` or `standard`) and retain its counts/reasons for the
   418	report. Full mode is always `standard`.
   419	
   420	Next plan cache use and dispatch. Bind `CONTRACT_VERSION` from the installed plugin's version
   421	metadata (the verifier prompt/agent/gate contract version; never invent a per-run value) and run:
   422	`python3 "$SD/scripts/plan-dispatch.py" --run-dir "$RUN_DIR" --runid "$RUNID" --repo-root "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR" --config "$CFG" --expect-config-sha "$CONFIG_SHA" --history "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/state/docaudit-history.json" --impact-json "$RUN_DIR/impact.json" --baseline-sha "$EFFECTIVE_BASELINE_SHA" --mode "$MODE" --contract-version "$CONTRACT_VERSION" --evidence "$EVIDENCE"`.
   423	If this reports `sealed-history-mismatch`, apply the stopping rule with `--taint-observed history --observed-by plan-dispatch.py`; do not continue to start or seal the run.
   424	Replace `EVIDENCE` with stdout unchanged. Parse `$RUN_DIR/dispatch.json` and bind `DISPATCH[]`,
   425	`CACHED[]`, and `HISTORY_STATUS`; cache qualification is deterministic and cached verdicts are
   426	written by `plan-dispatch.py` without an LLM. Never send `CACHED[]` to either verifier backend.
   427	
   428	Create the unsealed manifest with:
   429	`python3 "$SD/scripts/start-run.py" --run-dir "$RUN_DIR" --runid "$RUNID" --repo-root "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR" --impact-json "$RUN_DIR/impact.json" --dispatch-json "$RUN_DIR/dispatch.json" --run-class "$RUN_CLASS" --mode "$MODE" --config "$CFG" --expect-config-sha "$CONFIG_SHA" --evidence "$EVIDENCE"`.
   430	Again replace `EVIDENCE` with complete stdout. Parse `manifest.json` for `phase3Backend`, the
   431	codex-only `phase3CodexTimeoutSeconds`, `phase4Required`, `preflightRequired`, `digestExclude[]`,
   432	and the dispatch/cached partition, but do not hand-author
   433	the manifest or evidence. The manifest fixes the impacted set, HEAD, run class, cache partition,
   434	and Phase-4 requirement; the Phase-5 gate refuses any mismatch. These are **unsealed values for
   435	Phase 2 only**; do not use any variable bound by this raw parse after Phase 2. In particular,
   436	`preflightRequired` belongs only to Phase 0.5 and has already been consumed before this raw parse;
   437	it is not rebound after sealing.
   438	
   439	## Phase 3 — change-impact verification (sealed backend)
   440	Re-derive `CONFIG_SHA` from `EVIDENCE` as specified above.
   441	Seal the run before selecting or starting either verifier backend:
   442	`python3 "$SD/scripts/seal-run.py" --run-dir "$RUN_DIR" --repo-root "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR" --evidence "$EVIDENCE"`.
   443	On success replace `EVIDENCE` with its complete stdout; its `digest` and updated `manifest` are
   444	the trusted seal. Exit 5 means the HEAD or complete change set drifted after Phase 1: run
   445	`python3 "$SD/scripts/open-run.py" --run-base "$RUN_BASE" --repo-root "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR" --anchor-path "$ANCHOR_PATH" --release --runid "$RUNID"`,
   446	stop, and say “Phase 1 以降にソースが変わりました。監査を再実行してください。” Do not launch either verifier backend and do not calculate a replacement digest by hand.
   447	Any other non-zero exit, except exit 7 or stderr containing `sealed-config-mismatch` (which must
   448	follow the stopping rule above with `--taint-observed config --observed-by seal-run.py` and must not release the run): run
   449	`python3 "$SD/scripts/open-run.py" --run-base "$RUN_BASE" --repo-root "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR" --anchor-path "$ANCHOR_PATH" --release --runid "$RUNID"`,
   450	report `seal-run:` stderr, stop without calling `read-manifest.py`, and do not launch either verifier backend.
   451	
   452	Immediately verify and read that exact sealed manifest once:
   453	`SEALED_MANIFEST="$(python3 "$SD/scripts/read-manifest.py" --run-dir "$RUN_DIR" --evidence "$EVIDENCE")"`.
   454	If `read-manifest.py` fails, run
   455	`python3 "$SD/scripts/open-run.py" --run-base "$RUN_BASE" --repo-root "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR" --anchor-path "$ANCHOR_PATH" --release --runid "$RUNID"`,
   456	then stop without launching a verifier. Parse only `SEALED_MANIFEST` and bind:
   457	`SEALED_PHASE3_BACKEND="$(python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(json.loads(sys.argv[1])["phase3Backend"])' "$SEALED_MANIFEST")"`,
   458	`SEALED_PHASE3_CODEX_TIMEOUT_SECONDS="$(python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(json.loads(sys.argv[1]).get("phase3CodexTimeoutSeconds", ""))' "$SEALED_MANIFEST")"`,
   459	`SEALED_RUN_CLASS="$(python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(json.loads(sys.argv[1])["runClass"])' "$SEALED_MANIFEST")"`,
   460	`SEALED_PHASE4_REQUIRED="$(python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(str(json.loads(sys.argv[1])["phase4Required"]).lower())' "$SEALED_MANIFEST")"`,
   461	`SEALED_DIGEST_EXCLUDES="$(python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(json.dumps(json.loads(sys.argv[1])["digestExclude"], separators=(",", ":")))' "$SEALED_MANIFEST")"`,
   462	`SEALED_DISPATCH="$(python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(json.dumps(json.loads(sys.argv[1])["dispatch"], separators=(",", ":")))' "$SEALED_MANIFEST")"`,
   463	`SEALED_CACHED="$(python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(json.dumps(json.loads(sys.argv[1])["cached"], separators=(",", ":")))' "$SEALED_MANIFEST")"`, and
   464	`SEALED_PROVENANCE="$(python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(json.dumps(json.loads(sys.argv[1])["provenance"], separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True))' "$SEALED_MANIFEST")"`.
   465	Every manifest-derived value used from this point onward must come from `SEALED_MANIFEST`; never
   466	reuse a Phase-2 manifest variable.
   467	
   468	Use only sealed `manifest.phase3Backend`, rebound as `SEALED_PHASE3_BACKEND`, to select the
   469	verifier path. When it is `workflow`, immediately
   470	before fan-out refresh mdq whenever `MDQ_AVAILABLE` is true: re-run the same two-part preflight as
   471	Phase 0 — first `MDQ_PROBE_JSON="$(bash "$SD/scripts/mdq-index.sh" --config "$CFG" --expect-config-sha "$CONFIG_SHA" --repo-root "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR")"`,
   472	re-parse its JSON, and re-bind `MDQ_AVAILABLE`/`MDQ_BIN`; then, if it is still available, run
   473	`MDQ_HEALTH_PROBE_JSON="$(cd "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR" && python3 "$SD/scripts/mdq-health.py" --bin "$MDQ_BIN")"` and re-bind
   474	`MDQ_HEALTHY`/`MDQ_CHUNKS`/`MDQ_STATUS`. If either refresh step fails, or the health probe is
   475	unhealthy, re-bind `MDQ_AVAILABLE=false` when indexing is unavailable and always bind
   476	`MDQ_HEALTHY=false`; use mdq in fan-out only when both values are true, otherwise use grep-degrade.
   477	Bind the refresh failure detail for the Phase-5 mdq status line. Phase 0 establishes the initial
   478	index; this repeat is the freshness guarantee immediately before fan-out. When
   479	`SEALED_PHASE3_BACKEND` is `codex`, skip this refresh: the dispatcher deliberately never uses mdq
   480	and supplies grep-degrade instructions to every Codex process.
   481	After the Phase-3 indexing refresh, save the refreshed index JSON as `MDQ_PROBE_JSON` and record it:
   482	`printf '%s' "$MDQ_PROBE_JSON" | python3 "$SD/scripts/probe-record.py" --repo-root "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR" --runid "$RUNID" --evidence "$EVIDENCE" --seam indexing --stdin >/dev/null || echo "⚠ probe-record: indexing not recorded [non-blocking]"`.
   483	When its health probe ran, save its exact JSON as `MDQ_HEALTH_PROBE_JSON` and record it:
   484	`printf '%s' "$MDQ_HEALTH_PROBE_JSON" | python3 "$SD/scripts/probe-record.py" --repo-root "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR" --runid "$RUNID" --evidence "$EVIDENCE" --seam mdqHealth --stdin >/dev/null || echo "⚠ probe-record: mdqHealth not recorded [non-blocking]"`.
   485	
   486	If `SEALED_DISPATCH[]` is empty, do not launch either backend. Send the literal empty array `[]` to
   487	`python3 "$SD/scripts/write-evidence.py" --run-dir "$RUN_DIR" --name returns --stdin --evidence "$EVIDENCE"`
   488	and replace `EVIDENCE` with stdout; `returns` must always be a real evidence file and can never use
   489	the `none` sentinel.
   490	
   491	If `SEALED_DISPATCH[]` is non-empty and `SEALED_PHASE3_BACKEND` is `codex`, select
   492	`PHASE3_CODEX_MODEL=gpt-5.6-luna` for `SEALED_RUN_CLASS=light` or
   493	`PHASE3_CODEX_MODEL=gpt-5.6-terra` for `SEALED_RUN_CLASS=standard`, then run:
   494	`python3 "$SD/scripts/codex-dispatch.py" --run-dir "$RUN_DIR" --repo-root "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR" --model "$PHASE3_CODEX_MODEL" --effort medium --timeout-seconds "$SEALED_PHASE3_CODEX_TIMEOUT_SECONDS" --concurrency 4 --evidence "$EVIDENCE"`.
   495	Parse and report its stdout summary `returnsPath`/`attempts`/`ok`/`failed`. The dispatcher performs
   496	up to three cumulative attempts, preserves every failed assignment as a null return row, and
   497	publishes only validated verdicts. Send its complete `$RUN_DIR/returns.json` through
   498	`python3 "$SD/scripts/write-evidence.py" --run-dir "$RUN_DIR" --name returns --stdin --evidence "$EVIDENCE" < "$RUN_DIR/returns.json"`
   499	and replace `EVIDENCE` with stdout. Then run
   500	`python3 "$SD/scripts/check-verdicts.py" --run-dir "$RUN_DIR" --impact-json "$RUN_DIR/impact.json" --returns`
   501	and retain the final `missing`, `returnMissing`, `mismatch`, `invalid`, `duplicates`, and
   502	`manifestMismatch` arrays for the report. Do not launch Workflow or perform an orchestrator retry
   503	on this path; the dispatcher already owns all three attempts.
   504	
   505	Codex absence, failed authentication, launch failure, non-zero exit, timeout, or invalid output is
   506	fail-closed. Never silently fall back to Workflow: the dispatcher retains null rows, retries to its
   507	limit, and the gate then REFUSES incomplete evidence. Tell the user to repair/install/authenticate
   508	Codex, or set `phase3Backend` back to `workflow` and rerun the audit.
   509	
   510	If `SEALED_DISPATCH[]` is non-empty and `SEALED_PHASE3_BACKEND` is `workflow`, launch
   511	`Workflow({scriptPath: "$SD/references/workflow-template.js", args: {repoRoot: CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR, changeSummary, impacted: SEALED_DISPATCH entries with provenance from SEALED_PROVENANCE, verifierModel: SEALED_RUN_CLASS == "light" ? "haiku" : "sonnet", mdqAvailable: MDQ_AVAILABLE, mdqHealthy: MDQ_HEALTHY, cmAvailable: CM_AVAILABLE, axAvailable: AX_AVAILABLE, symbolGraphAvailable: SYMBOL_GRAPH_AVAILABLE, runId: RUNID, runDir: RUN_DIR, scriptsDir: "$SD/scripts"}})`.
   512	Pass dispatch entries only — never cached paths. The template preserves two runtime-dependent
   513	facts: Workflow `args` may arrive as a JSON string, and `agentType` is always plugin-namespaced
   514	(`docaudit:doc-impact-verifier-light` for Haiku or `docaudit:doc-impact-verifier` for Sonnet),
   515	never a bare name and never selected through `opts.model` precedence.
   516	`runId`/`runDir`/`scriptsDir` are REQUIRED. Each verifier first persists its own runid-stamped
   517	verdict via the supplied `write-verdict.py --run-dir ... --out ... --runid ... --path ...
   518	--verdict ...` command (`--source` is omitted, so it is a verifier record), then returns a
   519	structured verdict. Do not write verifier files from the orchestrator.
   520	
   521	For Workflow attempt 1, add `attempt:1` to every template return and append the whole returned array to an
   522	in-memory cumulative returns array. The template itself always emits one element per assignment:
   523	`{assignedPath,returnedPath,verdict,rationale,suggestion}`, normalizing both a null agent return and
   524	a null slot returned by `parallel()` to null fields. Therefore, when Workflow returns an array,
   525	element omission cannot occur. If Workflow throws before it can return an array at all, represent
   526	every assignment from that attempt with the same `assignedPath` and null return fields so the failed
   527	attempt remains explicit.
   528	Write the complete cumulative array (replace, never append the file) through
   529	`python3 "$SD/scripts/write-evidence.py" --run-dir "$RUN_DIR" --name returns --stdin --evidence "$EVIDENCE"`
   530	and replace `EVIDENCE` with stdout. Then run
   531	`python3 "$SD/scripts/check-verdicts.py" --run-dir "$RUN_DIR" --impact-json "$RUN_DIR/impact.json" --returns`
   532	and parse `missing`, `returnMissing`, `mismatch`, `invalid`, `duplicates`, and
   533	`manifestMismatch`. `returns.json` is mandatory gate evidence, not progress-only display.
   534	
   535	On the Workflow path, re-dispatch the union `missing ∪ returnMissing ∪ mismatch`, preserving provenance, at most twice
   536	after the initial attempt. Attempts 2 and 3 ALWAYS set `verifierModel:"sonnet"`, including retries
   537	after partial nulls or a Workflow throw. After each attempt, add its attempt number, rewrite the
   538	complete cumulative returns array through `write-evidence.py`, replace `EVIDENCE`, and rerun
   539	`check-verdicts.py --returns`. The sealed digest is assumed unchanged and will be rechecked by the
   540	gate; do not use the retired ad-hoc tree-digest calculation. If an exceptional retry decision
   541	needs an early confirmation, call `tree-digest.py --repo-root "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR"
   542	--include-head` with exactly this command shape, repeating the sealed exclude argument once per
   543	entry:
   544	`python3 "$SD/scripts/tree-digest.py" --repo-root "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR" --include-head --exclude "$SEALED_DIGEST_EXCLUDE"`.
   545	Each `SEALED_DIGEST_EXCLUDE` must be an entry read from `SEALED_DIGEST_EXCLUDES[]`; require
   546	the result to equal `EVIDENCE.digest`. Never broaden those excludes. After three total attempts,
   547	continue to Phase 4 with incomplete evidence so the deterministic gate can REFUSE it. The Codex
   548	path likewise continues after its final checker result. Record the final checker arrays for the
   549	report. (Built-in `/code-review` and `/security-review` cannot run
   550	inside Workflow; they remain in Phase 4.)
   551	
   552	## Phase 4 — existing layers + reviews (main loop, sequential)
   553	Re-derive `CONFIG_SHA` from `EVIDENCE` as specified above. Bind the Phase-4 values only through sealed getters:
   554	`DOC_AUDIT_COMMANDS_P4_JSON="$(python3 "$SD/scripts/sealed_config.py" --config "$CFG" --expect-sha "$CONFIG_SHA" --get docAuditCommands --default null)"`.
   555	`BOUNDARY_COMMAND="$(python3 "$SD/scripts/sealed_config.py" --config "$CFG" --expect-sha "$CONFIG_SHA" --get boundaryCommand --default null --raw)"`.
   556	`REVIEW_COMMANDS_JSON="$(python3 "$SD/scripts/sealed_config.py" --config "$CFG" --expect-sha "$CONFIG_SHA" --get reviewCommands --default '{}')"`.
   557	Global gate: run this phase's delegated checks **iff** `SEALED_PHASE4_REQUIRED` (parsed from
   558	`SEALED_MANIFEST.phase4Required`) is true. Do not re-derive this decision from impacted/SSOT/mode
   559	in the orchestrator. Apply the branch as:
   560	`if "$SEALED_PHASE4_REQUIRED"; then <run Phase 4>; fi`.
   561	1. From config `docAuditCommands`, run `existence` then `semantic` then `format`
   562	   (e.g. `/check-docs`, `doc-lint`, `/review-docs`) — whole-tree (no per-file arg).
   563	   Invoke each exactly as the config value names it (a skill like `doc-lint` is
   564	   invoked by name, not with a leading slash). A command classified `invalid` by Phase 0.5 is never
   565	   invoked here either; retain its pre-flight FAIL finding and run the built-in generic layer for
   566	   that layer instead, using the same fallback as an unavailable command. **Fallback:** if `docAuditCommands`
   567	   is absent, or a given layer's command is unavailable in this environment, run the
   568	   built-in generic layer instead:
   569	   `python3 "$SD/scripts/generic-layers.py" --layer <format|existence|semantic> --config "$CFG" --expect-config-sha "$CONFIG_SHA" --repo-root "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR"`
   570	   (in incremental mode you may add `--paths -` and pipe the impacted-doc list to scope it;
   571	   the semantic layer always scans the full repo for orphan-reference resolution regardless).
   572	   Fold its `findings[]` into the verdict: `severity:"FAIL"` -> NEEDS FIX, `severity:"WARN"` -> report only.
   573	   For the stamped `doc-lint` template in `installed`, and any adjusted command whose adjustment
   574	   explicitly adopted that contract, parse strict `path:line - FAIL|WARN - message` finding lines and
   575	   fold those severities. For every other model-driven delegated command, fold every reported finding
   576	   regardless of layout, normalizing `FAIL`/`HIGH`/`CRITICAL` to blocking and `WARN`/`MEDIUM`/`LOW`/`INFO`
   577	   to non-blocking. A final `VERDICT` line, when present, is a consistency check only; missing,
   578	   ambiguous, or contradictory output adds a non-blocking WARN and `parsed:false`.
   579	2. If `boundaryCommand` set and gate open, run it.
   580	3. Handle `reviewCommands.code` (e.g. `/code-review high`) on the working diff, then
   581	   `reviewCommands.security` (e.g. `/security-review`). Normalize any
   582	   `/security-audit ...` request to `/security-review`. In an interactive session,
   583	   before the gate and only once, use AskUserQuestion to offer running the configured
   584	   `/code-review` command. If the user chooses it, end the turn with: “Run
   585	   `/code-review <configured effort>` and, when complete, enter ‘continue the audit’.” Write
   586	   the Phase-5 cross-turn state (`RUNID` and complete `EVIDENCE`) before ending.
   587	   On resume, fold only findings visibly present in the same conversation into
   588	   the Phase-4 findings collection, normalizing `high`→`HIGH` and `medium`→`MEDIUM`; fold findings only
   589	   when they are visibly present. If completion of the review is confirmed, bind
   590	   `CODE_REVIEW_STATE=ran` even when findings are empty. If completion cannot be
   591	   confirmed, do not invent findings and use `CODE_REVIEW_STATE=not-model-invocable`.
   592	   In a non-interactive session, do not offer the question and use that expected
   593	   state directly. If execution reports the specific `disable-model-invocation`
   594	   block, bind `CODE_REVIEW_STATE=not-model-invocable` without WARN. Any other
   595	   unavailable or failed `reviewCommands.code` command (including project-specific
   596	   commands) is handled as before: skip and WARN. Then run `reviewCommands.security`.
   597	   `/code-review ultra` is non-blocking — never wait on a cloud run; default to the
   598	   configured effort. When `CM_AVAILABLE` is true and a review exposes its output as
   599	   capturable text/JSON or a file, do not read that raw output into context: reduce it
   600	   to its FAIL/WARN findings with `ctx_execute`/`ctx_batch_execute` in the sandbox and
   601	   fold only the distilled findings into the verdict (non-blocking; degrade to reading
   602	   the output directly when context-mode is absent).
   603	
   604	   After `/security-review`, run the **codex review** (the fourth, adversarial review —
   605	   the one seam among mdq/context-mode/ax/codex whose findings CAN affect the verdict;
   606	   see Guardrails). In incremental mode, bind `BASELINE_OK` to `true` only when
   607	   `git rev-parse --verify "$BASELINE_SHA^{commit}"` succeeds, otherwise `false`. In full mode,
   608	   do not run `rev-parse`; bind `BASELINE_OK=false`. Then run the
   609	   deterministic table before constructing a prompt or invoking Codex:
   610	   Bind `HISTORY_SHA` and `WORKTREE_DIGEST` from the current `EVIDENCE.history` and sealed manifest `worktreeDigest`, then run
   611	   `CODEX_REVIEW_PLAN="$(python3 "$SD/scripts/codex-review-plan.py" --mode "$MODE" --config "$CFG" --expect-config-sha "$CONFIG_SHA" --repo-root "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR" --available "$CODEX_REVIEW_AVAILABLE" --available-reason "$CODEX_REVIEW_REASON" --baseline-ok "$BASELINE_OK" --history "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/state/docaudit-history.json" --expect-history-sha "$HISTORY_SHA" --worktree-digest "$WORKTREE_DIGEST")"`.
   612	   Parse and bind its `action`, `state`, `promptVariant`, `carryForward`, `carryForwardSha`, and `reason`. When `action=skip` or
   613	   `action=not-active`, bind the returned `state` to `CODEX_REVIEW_STATE`, fold no findings, and
   614	   do not invoke `codex exec`. Do not repeat full-mode or baseline validity decisions outside this
   615	   table.
   616	
   617	   Only when `action=run`, bind `CODEX_MODEL` on every invocation. If config has a non-empty
   618	   `codexReview.model`, use it and mark the choice explicit. Otherwise use `gpt-5.6-luna` for
   619	   `SEALED_RUN_CLASS=light` and `gpt-5.6-terra` for `SEALED_RUN_CLASS=standard`. Every invocation
   620	   also uses `-c model_reasoning_effort=medium`. Write the review prompt with the Write tool, as
   621	   its own step, to `$RUN_DIR/codex-review-prompt.txt`. For `promptVariant=diff`, use the current
   622	   explicit "review the diff between `$BASELINE_SHA` and HEAD" scope plus the Phase-2

   374	Do NOT pass `--full` to this script (it only accepts `--config`/`--repo-root`; an unknown flag makes it `exit 2`). `--full` is a skill-level argument only: after parsing the script output, if the skill was invoked with `--full`, set the effective `MODE` to `full` in memory.    675	Re-derive `CONFIG_SHA` from `EVIDENCE` as specified above.
   676	Phase-3 verdicts (`$RUN_DIR/verdicts/`) and required Phase-4 findings
   677	(`$RUN_DIR/phase4.json`, absent only with the valid `none` sentinel) are already on disk. **You do

   683	
   684	Within each status-line table the first matching bullet wins: the whole-record unknown bullet (when the table has one) comes first, the invalid-config bullet second, then the remaining states; for codex-review use invalid-config → review-state-not-recorded → probe-record-unavailable → 4-way.

   695	warning and continues; a failed read makes all seven status lines unknown; neither case changes
   696	the verdict. A failed resume re-record for webExtract/codexReview additionally forces its own line

   709	
   710	| placeholder | exact occurrences | gate-rendered value |
   711	|---|---:|---|
   712	| `{{GATE_VERDICT}}` | 1 | final verdict |
   713	| `{{GATE_REASON}}` | 0 or 1 | REFUSED reason; `"n/a"` on success |
   714	| `{{GATE_COUNTS}}` | 1 | counts; `"n/a"` on REFUSED |
   715	| `{{GATE_HISTORY_STATUS}}` | 1 | history status; `"n/a"` on REFUSED |
   716	| `{{GATE_WARNINGS}}` | 1 | gate warning codes |
   717	| `{{GATE_SIBLING_SCAN}}` | 1 | sibling scan; `"n/a"` on REFUSED |
   718	| `{{GATE_ANCHOR_WRITTEN}}` | 1 | whether the anchor was written |
   719	| `{{GATE_REPORT_DATE}}` | 2 | sealed date for front matter `created` and `updated` |
   720	
   721	`{{GATE_WARNINGS}}` includes only warnings known before report publication. For warnings discovered
   722	during publication (`reportDurabilityUnknown`, `reportWriteError`, `reportStatusUpdateFailed`, or
   723	`lockReleaseFailed`), the gate stdout and `last_run.reportStatus` are authoritative.
   724	
   725	Pass that UTF-8 body on stdin to
   726	`python3 "$SD/scripts/write-template.py" --repo-root "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR" --runid "$RUNID"`.
   727	The helper binds the template to the run ledger; never write `report-template.md`, its receipt, or
   728	the final `reportPath` directly. If the body must be regenerated, invoke the same helper with the
   729	explicit `--replace` flag and pass the complete replacement body on stdin. If `reportPath` is not
   730	configured, do not create a template; the gate completes reportless.
   731	
   732	After the helper succeeds, invoke the gate:
   733	`python3 "$SD/scripts/decide-verdict.py" --run-dir "$RUN_DIR" --repo-root "$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR" --config "$CFG" --anchor-path "$ANCHOR_PATH" --runid "$RUNID" --expect-json "$EVIDENCE"`.
   734	The gate validates the sealed immutable evidence snapshot, updates persistent state when allowed,
   735	and publishes the report while holding the lock, then releases the lock. Parse stdout `verdict`
   736	(`CONSISTENT`, `NEEDS_FIX`, or `REFUSED`), `reason`, `counts`, `historyStatus`, `warnings`,
   737	`siblingScan` (always an object for CONSISTENT and NEEDS_FIX), `reportPath`, `reportStatus`, and
   738	`codexReview:{state,required,degraded}`. When Codex review is degraded, the gate renders
   739	`{{GATE_VERDICT}}` as `CONSISTENT (codex-review did not run: <state>)` while keeping stdout
   740	`verdict` equal to `CONSISTENT`;
   741	include `counts.verdictFlipsUnchangedContent` and
   742	`counts.verdictFlipsUnchangedContentSameChangeSet` and `counts.phase4FlipsUnchangedContent` in the existing report counts line. The Phase-4 counter compares only records with the same worktreeDigest, contractVersion, configSha, and carryForwardSha and is a warning, never a verdict input;
   743	never replace any of them with an orchestrator judgment. Report stdout `reportPath`, `warnings`,
   744	and `reportStatus` to the user. `reportPath` exists only after successful publication, and
   745	`reportStatus` is omitted when a pre-lock or non-owned REFUSED path wrote no `last_run` state.
   746	`CONSISTENT` means the anchor advanced, `NEEDS_FIX` means blocking evidence was found and the
   747	anchor did not advance, and exit 3 `REFUSED` means the run is invalid. Never override
   748	`NEEDS_FIX`/`REFUSED`, hand-write an anchor or report, fabricate evidence, or retry the gate with a
   749	modified expectation. If a REFUSED `reason` is config change/unaccepted config, add: inspect
   750	`git diff .claude/doc-audit.json`, restore or approve it, then explicitly re-run with
   751	`/docaudit:audit --accept-config`.
   752	
   753	After invoking the gate, orchestrator lock release is required on exactly two recovery paths:
   754	(i) a pre-lock REFUSED caused by invalid required EVIDENCE or invalid invocation identity, where
   755	the gate could not validate and release the lock — run the matching
   756	`open-run.py --release --runid "$RUNID"`; and (ii) stdout `warnings` contains
   757	`lockReleaseFailed` — after the gate exits, run the same ownership-checking release command. If
   758	either release fails, stop and tell the user to inspect the holder and run
   759	`/docaudit:audit --break-lock`. A true fd/path/expected-inode mismatch after lock validation is
   760	non-owned: do not call `--release`; stop and give the same `--break-lock` guidance.

    16	SEVERITY_RANK = {"CRITICAL": 0, "HIGH": 1, "MEDIUM": 2, "LOW": 3}
    17	SAFE_ASCII_PATH_CHARS = frozenset(
    18	    "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789._/@+-")

    25	def canonical_bytes(value):
    26	    return json.dumps(
    27	        value, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=True
    28	    ).encode("utf-8")

    31	def load_history(path, expected_sha):
    32	    raw = None
    33	    if os.path.isfile(path):
    34	        with open(path, "rb") as handle:
    35	            raw = handle.read()
    36	    observed_sha = sha256_bytes(raw) if raw is not None else "none"
    37	    if observed_sha != expected_sha:
    38	        raise RuntimeError(
    39	            f"sealed-history-mismatch: expected {expected_sha} observed {observed_sha}")
    40	    if raw is None:
    41	        return [], []
    42	    try:
    43	        _entries, phase4_runs, warnings = parse_history_document(
    44	            json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8")))
    45	        return phase4_runs, warnings
    46	    except (UnicodeError, ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
    47	        return [], [f"carry-forward skipped: history corrupt ({exc})"]
    48	
    49	
    50	def carry_forward(repo_root, phase4_runs, worktree_digest):
    51	    source = next((
    52	        record for record in reversed(phase4_runs)
    53	        if record["worktreeDigest"] != worktree_digest
    54	    ), None)
    55	    if source is None:
    56	        return None, "none"
    57	    files = []
    58	    for finding in sorted(
    59	            source["findings"],
    60	            key=lambda item: (SEVERITY_RANK[item["severity"]], item["file"])):
    61	        path = finding["file"]
    62	        if not all(char in SAFE_ASCII_PATH_CHARS or ord(char) >= 128
    63	                   for char in path):
    64	            continue
    65	        try:
    66	            path = validate_repo_path(repo_root, path)
    67	        except ValueError:
    68	            continue
    69	        files.append({"file": path, "severity": finding["severity"]})
    70	        if len(files) == 50:
    71	            break
    72	    if not files:
    73	        return None, "none"
    74	    value = {"files": files}
    75	    return value, "sha256:" + hashlib.sha256(canonical_bytes(value)).hexdigest()
    76

   108	    if not isinstance(codex_config, dict):

   119	                  "promptVariant": None, "reason": args.available_reason}
   120	    elif args.mode == "full" and required:
   121	        result = {"action": "run", "state": None, "promptVariant": "full",
   122	                  "reason": "ready"}
   123	    elif args.mode == "full":
   124	        result = {"action": "skip", "state": "skipped-full-run",
   125	                  "promptVariant": None, "reason": "full-run-without-required"}
   126	    elif parse_bool(args.baseline_ok):
   127	        result = {"action": "run", "state": None, "promptVariant": "diff",
   128	                  "reason": "ready"}
   129	    else:
   130	        result = {"action": "skip", "state": "ref-invalid",
   131	                  "promptVariant": None, "reason": "baseline-ref-invalid"}

   136	        raise AssertionError("codex-review plan emitted an unknown state")

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Read a JSON config once and verify its sealed SHA-256 before use."""
     3	
     4	import argparse
     5	import hashlib
     6	import json
     7	import os
     8	import re
     9	import sys
    10	
    11	
    12	SHA_RE = re.compile(r"sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}")
    13	_MISSING = object()
    14	
    15	
    16	class SealedConfigMismatch(Exception):
    17	    """The bytes read from config do not match the expected seal."""
    18	
    19	    def __init__(self, expected_sha, observed_sha):
    20	        super().__init__(expected_sha, observed_sha)
    21	        self.expected_sha = expected_sha
    22	        self.observed_sha = observed_sha
    23	
    24	    def __str__(self):
    25	        return (f"sealed-config-mismatch: expected {self.expected_sha} "
    26	                f"observed {self.observed_sha}")
    27	
    28	
    29	def load_sealed_config(path, expected_sha, with_signature=False):
    30	    """Return verified bytes and JSON, optionally with the read fd's signature."""
    31	    if not isinstance(expected_sha, str) or not SHA_RE.fullmatch(expected_sha):
    32	        raise ValueError("expected config sha must be sha256:<64 lowercase hex>")
    33	    fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW)
    34	    try:
    35	        chunks = []
    36	        while True:
    37	            chunk = os.read(fd, 1024 * 1024)
    38	            if not chunk:
    39	                break
    40	            chunks.append(chunk)
    41	        raw = b"".join(chunks)
    42	        observed_sha = "sha256:" + hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
    43	        signature = None
    44	        if with_signature:
    45	            info = os.fstat(fd)
    46	            try:
    47	                path_info = os.lstat(path)
    48	            except OSError as exc:
    49	                raise SealedConfigMismatch(expected_sha, observed_sha) from exc
    50	            if path_info.st_ino != info.st_ino:
    51	                raise SealedConfigMismatch(expected_sha, observed_sha)
    52	            signature = (info.st_ino, info.st_size, info.st_mtime_ns)
    53	    finally:
    54	        os.close(fd)
    55	    if observed_sha != expected_sha:
    56	        raise SealedConfigMismatch(expected_sha, observed_sha)
    57	    doc = json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))
    58	    if with_signature:
    59	        return raw, doc, signature
    60	    return raw, doc
    61	
    62	
    63	def dotted_get(value, dotted_key):
    64	    parts = dotted_key.split(".")
    65	    if not dotted_key or any(not part for part in parts):
    66	        raise ValueError("--get requires a non-empty dotted key")
    67	    current = value
    68	    for part in parts:
    69	        if not isinstance(current, dict) or part not in current:
    70	            return _MISSING
    71	        current = current[part]
    72	    return current
    73	
    74	
    75	def main():
    76	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    77	    parser.add_argument("--config", required=True)
    78	    parser.add_argument("--expect-sha", required=True)
    79	    action = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)
    80	    action.add_argument("--print", dest="print_config", action="store_true")
    81	    action.add_argument("--get")
    82	    parser.add_argument("--default")
    83	    parser.add_argument("--raw", action="store_true")
    84	    args = parser.parse_args()
    85	
    86	    try:
    87	        if not SHA_RE.fullmatch(args.expect_sha):
    88	            raise ValueError("--expect-sha must be sha256:<64 lowercase hex>")
    89	        if args.print_config and (args.default is not None or args.raw):
    90	            raise ValueError("--default and --raw require --get")
    91	        default = _MISSING if args.default is None else json.loads(args.default)
    92	        _raw, doc = load_sealed_config(args.config, args.expect_sha)
    93	        if args.print_config:
    94	            value = doc
    95	        else:
    96	            value = dotted_get(doc, args.get)
    97	            if value is _MISSING:
    98	                value = None if default is _MISSING else default
    99	        if args.raw:
   100	            if value is None:
   101	                output = ""
   102	            elif isinstance(value, str):
   103	                output = value
   104	            else:
   105	                raise ValueError("--raw value must be a string or null")
   106	            sys.stdout.buffer.write((output + "\n").encode("utf-8"))
   107	        else:
   108	            rendered = json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=True, sort_keys=True) + "\n"
   109	            sys.stdout.buffer.write(rendered.encode("ascii"))
   110	        return 0
   111	    except SealedConfigMismatch as exc:
   112	        print(str(exc), file=sys.stderr)
   113	        return 7
   114	    except (OSError, UnicodeError, ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   115	        print(f"sealed-config: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
   116	        return 2
   117	
   118	
   119	if __name__ == "__main__":
   120	    raise SystemExit(main())

    86	def report_pattern(config):
    87	    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("change_set_sha", os.path.join(HERE, "change-set-sha.py"))
    88	    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    89	    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    90	    return module.report_pattern(config)

    93	def run_sibling_scan(payload, repo, timeout_s=30):
    94	    try:
    95	        proc = subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join(HERE, "sibling-scan.py"), "--stdin"],
    96	                              input=json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False), capture_output=True,
    97	                              text=True, timeout=timeout_s)
    98	        if proc.returncode:
    99	            return sibling_skipped("sibling scan exited " + str(proc.returncode))
   100	        value = json.loads(proc.stdout)
   101	        if not isinstance(value, dict):
   102	            return sibling_skipped("sibling scan returned invalid JSON")
   103	        return value
   104	    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   105	        return sibling_skipped("sibling scan timed out")
   106	    except (OSError, ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   107	        return sibling_skipped("sibling scan failed: " + str(exc))

   115	                   "phase4": phase4, "reportPattern": report_pattern(config)}
   116	        return run_sibling_scan(payload, repo)
   117	    except Exception as exc:

   121	def atomic(path, value):
   122	    raw = json_bytes(value)
   123	    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
   124	    fd, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".docaudit-state.", dir=os.path.dirname(path))
   125	    try:
   126	        with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as handle:
   127	            handle.write(raw)
   128	            handle.flush()
   129	            os.fsync(handle.fileno())
   130	        os.replace(temporary, path)
   131	    finally:
   132	        if os.path.exists(temporary):
   133	            os.unlink(temporary)

   139	    except (UnicodeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   140	        raise Refused(f"{label} is not valid JSON: {exc}") from exc

   144	def read_once(path, label):
   145	    try:
   146	        with open(path, "rb") as handle:
   147	            return handle.read()
   148	    except OSError as exc:
   149	        raise Refused(f"{label} cannot be read: {exc}") from exc

   152	def read_state_once(path, label):
   153	    try:
   154	        fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW)
   155	        try:
   156	            chunks = []
   157	            while True:
   158	                chunk = os.read(fd, 65536)
   159	                if not chunk:
   160	                    break
   161	                chunks.append(chunk)
   162	            info = os.fstat(fd)
   163	            if os.stat(path, follow_symlinks=False).st_ino != info.st_ino:
   164	                raise Refused(f"{label} was replaced while being read")
   165	            signature = (info.st_ino, info.st_size, info.st_mtime_ns)
   166	            return b"".join(chunks), signature
   167	        finally:
   168	            os.close(fd)
   169	    except OSError as exc:
   170	        raise Refused(f"{label} cannot be read: {exc}") from exc

   178	def state_unchanged(path, signature):
   179	    if signature is None:
   180	        return not os.path.exists(path)
   181	    try:
   182	        return state_signature(path) == signature
   183	    except OSError:
   184	        return False

   187	def verify_sha(raw, expected, label):
   188	    actual = sha256_bytes(raw)
   189	    if actual != expected:
   190	        raise Refused(f"{label} sha mismatch")

   193	def impact_provenance(value):
   194	    if not isinstance(value, dict) or not isinstance(value.get("impacted"), list):
   195	        raise Refused("provenance mismatch")
   196	    provenance = {}
   197	    for item in value["impacted"]:
   198	        if (not isinstance(item, dict) or not isinstance(item.get("path"), str)
   199	                or item["path"] in provenance or "provenance" not in item):
   200	            raise Refused("provenance mismatch")
   201	        provenance[item["path"]] = item["provenance"]
   202	    return provenance
   203	
   204	
   205	def validate_provenance(manifest, impacted, from_impact):
   206	    provenance = manifest.get("provenance")
   207	    if not isinstance(provenance, dict) or set(provenance) != set(impacted):
   208	        raise Refused("provenance mismatch")
   209	    if provenance != from_impact:
   210	        raise Refused("provenance mismatch")
   211	    for path in sorted(provenance):
   212	        value = provenance[path]
   213	        if not isinstance(value, str) or value not in VALID_PROVENANCE:
   214	            raise Refused(f"provenance enum violation: {path}={value}")
   215

   217	def verify_audit_scope_at_barrier(repo, config, expected_sha):
   218	    if expected_sha is None:
   219	        return
   220	    try:
   221	        metadata = config.get("auditScope")
   222	        if (not isinstance(expected_sha, str) or not isinstance(metadata, dict)
   223	                or not isinstance(metadata.get("path"), str)):
   224	            raise ValueError("invalid auditScope metadata")
   225	        scope_rel = validate_repo_path(repo, metadata["path"])
   226	        scope_raw = read_once(os.path.join(repo, scope_rel), "audit scope")
   227	        if sha256_bytes(scope_raw) != expected_sha:
   228	            raise ValueError("sha mismatch")
   229	    except (OSError, ValueError, Refused) as exc:
   230	        raise Refused("audit-scope changed after seal") from exc

   233	def verdict_flip_counts(history_entries, additions, dispatched):
   234	    latest = {}
   235	    for entry in history_entries:
   236	        latest[entry["path"]] = entry
   237	    flips = 0
   238	    same_change_set = 0
   239	    dispatched = set(dispatched)
   240	    for current in additions:
   241	        if current["path"] not in dispatched:
   242	            continue
   243	        previous = latest.get(current["path"])
   244	        if (previous is not None
   245	                and previous["contentSha"] == current["contentSha"]
   246	                and previous["contractVersion"] == current["contractVersion"]
   247	                and previous.get("backend", "workflow") == current["backend"]
   248	                and previous["verdict"] != current["verdict"]):
   249	            flips += 1
   250	            if previous["changeSetSha"] == current["changeSetSha"]:
   251	                same_change_set += 1
   252	    return flips, same_change_set
   253	
   254	
   255	def validate_returns(value):
   256	    if not isinstance(value, list):
   257	        raise Refused("returns.json must be an array")
   258	    seen = set()
   259	    for index, item in enumerate(value):
   260	        if not isinstance(item, dict):
   261	            raise Refused(f"returns[{index}] is not an object")
   262	        attempt = item.get("attempt")
   263	        assigned = item.get("assignedPath")
   264	        if (isinstance(attempt, bool) or not isinstance(attempt, int) or not 1 <= attempt <= 3
   265	                or not isinstance(assigned, str)):
   266	            raise Refused(f"returns[{index}] has invalid attempt/assignedPath")
   267	        if (attempt, assigned) in seen:
   268	            raise Refused("returns contains duplicate (attempt,assignedPath)")
   269	        seen.add((attempt, assigned))
   270	        if item.get("returnedPath") is not None and not isinstance(item.get("returnedPath"), str):
   271	            raise Refused(f"returns[{index}].returnedPath is invalid")
   272	        if item.get("verdict") is not None and item.get("verdict") not in VALID_VERDICTS:
   273	            raise Refused(f"returns[{index}].verdict is invalid")
   274	
   275	
   276	def findings_fail(value):
   277	    if not isinstance(value, dict):
   278	        raise Refused("phase evidence must be an object")
   279	    findings = value.get("findings", [])
   280	    if not isinstance(findings, list):
   281	        raise Refused("phase findings must be an array")
   282	    blocking = False
   283	    for finding in findings:
   284	        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   285	            raise Refused("phase finding is not an object")
   286	        raw_severity = finding.get("severity")
   287	        if not isinstance(raw_severity, str) or not raw_severity.strip():
   288	            raise Refused("phase finding severity is missing")
   289	        severity = raw_severity.strip().upper()
   290	        if severity in FAIL_SEVERITIES:
   291	            blocking = True
   292	        elif severity not in {"PASS", "WARN", "MEDIUM", "LOW", "INFO"}:
   293	            raise Refused(f"unknown finding severity: {severity}")
   294	    if value.get("parsed") is False:
   295	        exit_code = value.get("exitCode", value.get("returncode", 0))
   296	        if isinstance(exit_code, int) and exit_code != 0:
   297	            return True
   298	        commands = value.get("commands", [])
   299	        if isinstance(commands, list) and any(isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("exitCode") not in (None, 0)
   300	                                              for item in commands):
   301	            return True
   302	    return blocking
   303	
   304	
   305	PHASE4_ELIGIBILITY = {
   306	    ("full", "full", "completed"): True,
   307	    ("full", "full", "execution-failed"): False,
   308	    ("full", None, "skipped-full-run"): False,
   309	    ("full", None, "not-active"): False,
   310	    ("incremental", "diff", "completed"): False,
   311	    ("incremental", "diff", "execution-failed"): False,
   312	    ("incremental", None, "ref-invalid"): False,
   313	    ("incremental", None, "not-active"): False,
   314	}
   315	
   316	
   317	def validate_phase4_contract(repo, manifest, phase4):
   318	    if not isinstance(phase4, dict):
   319	        raise Refused("phase4 evidence must be an object")
   320	    codex = phase4.get("codexReview")
   321	    if not isinstance(codex, dict):
   322	        raise Refused("codexReview evidence invalid: codexReview must be an object")
   323	    if "state" not in codex or "promptVariant" not in codex or "carryForwardSha" not in codex:
   324	        raise Refused("codexReview evidence invalid: required keys are missing")
   325	    state = codex["state"]
   326	    variant = codex["promptVariant"]
   327	    carry_sha = codex["carryForwardSha"]
   328	    if not isinstance(state, str):
   329	        raise Refused("codexReview evidence invalid: state must be a string")
   330	    if state not in CODEX_REVIEW_STATES:
   331	        raise Refused("codexReview evidence invalid: state is not recognized")
   332	    if variant not in ("full", "diff", None):
   333	        raise Refused("codexReview evidence invalid: promptVariant is not recognized")
   334	    if not isinstance(carry_sha, str):
   335	        raise Refused("codexReview evidence invalid: carryForwardSha must be a string")
   336	    key = (manifest.get("mode"), variant, state)
   337	    if key not in PHASE4_ELIGIBILITY:
   338	        raise Refused("codexReview evidence invalid: mode, promptVariant, and state conflict")
   339	    normalized = set()
   340	    unresolved = 0
   341	    findings = phase4.get("findings", [])
   342	    if not isinstance(findings, list):
   343	        raise Refused("phase findings must be an array")
   344	    for finding in findings:
   345	        if not isinstance(finding, dict) or finding.get("source") != "codex-review":
   346	            continue
   347	        value = finding.get("file")
   348	        if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
   349	            raise Refused("codex-review finding file is missing")
   350	        severity = finding.get("severity")
   351	        if not isinstance(severity, str) or severity.upper() not in PHASE4_SEVERITIES:
   352	            raise Refused("codex-review finding severity is invalid")
   353	        try:
   354	            path = normalize_finding_path(repo, value)
   355	        except ValueError:
   356	            path = None
   357	        if path is None:
   358	            unresolved += 1
   359	        else:
   360	            normalized.add((path, severity.upper()))
   361	    rank = {"CRITICAL": 4, "HIGH": 3, "MEDIUM": 2, "LOW": 1}
   362	    ordered = [{"file": path, "severity": severity}
   363	               for path, severity in sorted(normalized,
   364	                                            key=lambda item: (-rank[item[1]], item[0]))]
   365	    return PHASE4_ELIGIBILITY[key], codex, ordered, unresolved
   366	
   367	
   368	def phase4_flip_count(records, current):
   369	    if current.get("truncated"):
   370	        return 0
   371	    keys = ("worktreeDigest", "contractVersion", "configSha", "carryForwardSha")
   372	    previous = next((record for record in reversed(records)
   373	                     if not record.get("truncated")
   374	                     and all(record.get(key) == current.get(key) for key in keys)), None)
   375	    if previous is None:
   376	        return 0
   377	    old = {item["file"] for item in previous["findings"]
   378	           if item["severity"] in {"CRITICAL", "HIGH"}}
   379	    new = {item["file"] for item in current["findings"]
   380	           if item["severity"] in {"CRITICAL", "HIGH"}}
   381	    return len(old.symmetric_difference(new))
   382	
   383	
   384	def trim_phase4_runs(records):
   385	    if len(records) <= 5:
   386	        return records
   387	    latest_digest = records[-1]["worktreeDigest"]
   388	    keep_indexes = set(range(max(0, len(records) - 5), len(records)))
   389	    source_index = next((index for index in range(len(records) - 2, -1, -1)
   390	                         if records[index]["worktreeDigest"] != latest_digest), None)
   391	    if source_index is not None:
   392	        keep_indexes.add(source_index)
   393	    return [record for index, record in enumerate(records) if index in keep_indexes]
   394	
   395	
   396	def run_tree_digest(repo, manifest):
   397	    command = [sys.executable, os.path.join(HERE, "tree-digest.py"),
   398	               "--repo-root", repo, "--include-head"]
   399	    for item in manifest.get("digestExclude", []):
   400	        command.extend(["--exclude", item])
   401	    proc = subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True, text=True)
   402	    if proc.returncode:
   403	        raise Refused(proc.stderr.strip() or "tree-digest failed")
   404	    return json.loads(proc.stdout)["digest"]
   405	
   406	
   407	def lock_recheck(fd, lock_path, runid, expected_inode):
   408	    try:
   409	        inode_fd = os.fstat(fd).st_ino
   410	        inode_path = os.stat(lock_path, follow_symlinks=False).st_ino
   411	    except OSError as exc:
   412	        raise Refused(f"lock disappeared or changed: {exc}") from exc
   413	    os.lseek(fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
   414	    raw = os.read(fd, 65536)
   415	    holder = parse_json(raw, "lock")
   416	    if inode_fd != inode_path or inode_fd != expected_inode or holder.get("runid") != runid:
   417	        raise Refused("lock inode or runid mismatch")
   418	    return holder
   419	
   420	
   421	def validate_evidence(value):
   422	    if not isinstance(value, dict):
   423	        raise Refused("EVIDENCE is not an object")
   424	    if REQUIRED_EXPECT - set(value):
   425	        raise Refused("EVIDENCE required keys are missing")
   426	    if not isinstance(value["runid"], str) or not isinstance(value["runDir"], str):
   427	        raise Refused("EVIDENCE run identity has invalid types")
   428	    if (isinstance(value["lockIno"], bool) or not isinstance(value["lockIno"], int)
   429	            or value["lockIno"] <= 0):
   430	        raise Refused("EVIDENCE lockIno has invalid type")
   431	    if (isinstance(value["attempt"], bool) or not isinstance(value["attempt"], int)
   432	            or not 0 <= value["attempt"] <= 3):
   433	        raise Refused("EVIDENCE attempt has invalid type")
   434	    if value["historyStatus"] not in {"absent", "ok", "corrupt"}:
   435	        raise Refused("EVIDENCE historyStatus is invalid")
   436	    sha_fields = {"config", "dispatch", "manifest", "digest", "returns"}
   437	    optional_sha_fields = {"anchor", "preflight", "cached", "history", "phase4"}
   438	    sha_re = re.compile(r"^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$")
   439	    for key in sha_fields:
   440	        if not isinstance(value[key], str) or not sha_re.fullmatch(value[key]):
   441	            raise Refused(f"EVIDENCE {key} has invalid type")
   442	    for key in optional_sha_fields:
   443	        if not isinstance(value[key], str) or (value[key] != "none" and not sha_re.fullmatch(value[key])):
   444	            raise Refused(f"EVIDENCE {key} has invalid type")
   445	
   446	
   447	def validate_taint_evidence(value):
   448	    """Validate only identity/seal fields available immediately after open-run."""
   449	    if not isinstance(value, dict):
   450	        raise Refused("EVIDENCE is not an object")
   451	    if {"runid", "runDir", "lockIno", "config"} - set(value):
   452	        raise Refused("EVIDENCE taint identity fields are missing")
   453	    if not isinstance(value["runid"], str) or not isinstance(value["runDir"], str):
   454	        raise Refused("EVIDENCE run identity has invalid types")
   455	    if (isinstance(value["lockIno"], bool) or not isinstance(value["lockIno"], int)
   456	            or value["lockIno"] <= 0):
   457	        raise Refused("EVIDENCE lockIno has invalid type")
   458	    if (not isinstance(value["config"], str)
   459	            or not re.fullmatch(r"sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}", value["config"])):
   460	        raise Refused("EVIDENCE config has invalid type")
   461	
   462	
   463	def validate_report_rule(manifest, repo, runid):
   464	    report_date = manifest.get("reportDate")
   465	    if not isinstance(report_date, str):
   466	        raise Refused("sealed reportDate is missing")
   467	    try:
   468	        parsed = datetime.date.fromisoformat(report_date)
   469	        run_date = datetime.datetime.strptime(runid[:16], "%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ").date()
   470	    except ValueError as exc:
   471	        raise Refused("sealed reportDate is invalid") from exc
   472	    if parsed.isoformat() != report_date or parsed != run_date:
   473	        raise Refused("sealed reportDate does not match runid UTC date")
   474	    rule = manifest.get("reportCandidateRule")
   475	    if rule is None:
   476	        return None
   477	    if not isinstance(rule, dict) or set(rule) != {
   478	            "base", "suffixPrefix", "suffixSuffix", "suffixStart"}:
   479	        raise Refused("sealed report candidate rule is invalid")
   480	    if (not all(isinstance(rule[key], str) for key in ("base", "suffixPrefix", "suffixSuffix"))
   481	            or not isinstance(rule["suffixStart"], int)
   482	            or isinstance(rule["suffixStart"], bool)
   483	            or rule["suffixStart"] != 2
   484	            or rule["base"] != rule["suffixPrefix"] + rule["suffixSuffix"]
   485	            or report_date not in rule["base"]):
   486	        raise Refused("sealed report candidate rule is invalid")
   487	    try:
   488	        validate_repo_path(repo, rule["base"], must_exist=False)
   489	        validate_repo_path(repo, rule["suffixPrefix"] + "_02" + rule["suffixSuffix"],
   490	                           must_exist=False)
   491	    except ValueError as exc:
   492	        raise Refused(f"sealed report candidate rule is unsafe: {exc}") from exc
   493	    return rule
   494	
   495	
   496	def read_regular_bounded(path, label, maximum, missing_error):
   497	    try:
   498	        fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW)
   499	    except FileNotFoundError as exc:
   500	        raise missing_error(f"{label} is missing") from exc
   501	    except OSError as exc:
   502	        raise TemplateInvalid(f"{label} cannot be safely opened: {exc}") from exc
   503	    try:
   504	        info = os.fstat(fd)
   505	        if not stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode):
   506	            raise TemplateInvalid(f"{label} is not a regular file")
   507	        chunks = []
   508	        remaining = maximum + 1
   509	        while remaining:
   510	            chunk = os.read(fd, min(65536, remaining))
   511	            if not chunk:
   512	                break
   513	            chunks.append(chunk)
   514	            remaining -= len(chunk)
   515	        raw = b"".join(chunks)
   516	        if len(raw) > maximum:
   517	            raise TemplateInvalid(f"{label} exceeds its size limit")
   518	        return raw
   519	    finally:
   520	        os.close(fd)
   521	
   522	
   523	def load_report_template(run_dir):
   524	    receipt_raw = read_regular_bounded(
   525	        os.path.join(run_dir, "report-template.receipt.json"), "report receipt",
   526	        MAX_RECEIPT_BYTES, TemplateMissing)
   527	    try:
   528	        receipt = json.loads(receipt_raw.decode("utf-8"))
   529	    except (UnicodeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   530	        raise TemplateInvalid(f"report receipt is invalid: {exc}") from exc
   531	    if not isinstance(receipt, dict) or receipt.get("failed") is not False:
   532	        raise TemplateInvalid("report receipt does not record a successful helper invocation")
   533	    raw = read_regular_bounded(os.path.join(run_dir, "report-template.md"), "report template",
   534	                               MAX_TEMPLATE_BYTES, TemplateMissing)
   535	    expected_sha = "sha256:" + hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
   536	    if (receipt.get("sha256") != expected_sha or receipt.get("bytes") != len(raw)
   537	            or isinstance(receipt.get("bytes"), bool)):
   538	        raise TemplateInvalid("report receipt does not match the template")
   539	    try:
   540	        return raw.decode("utf-8")
   541	    except UnicodeError as exc:
   542	        raise TemplateInvalid(f"report template is not valid UTF-8: {exc}") from exc
   543	
   544	
   545	def safe_json(value):
   546	    rendered = json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
   547	    if any(char in BIDI_CONTROLS for char in rendered):
   548	        raise TemplateInvalid("report replacement contains a bidirectional control character")
   549	    return (rendered.replace("\u2028", "\\u2028").replace("\u2029", "\\u2029")
   550	            .replace("<", "\\u003c").replace(">", "\\u003e").replace("&", "\\u0026"))
   551	
   552	
   553	def render_report(template, verdict, report_date, *, reason=None, counts=None,
   554	                  history_status=None, warnings=None, sibling=None, anchor_written=False):
   555	    found = TOKEN_RE.findall(template)
   556	    if any(token not in TOKEN_COUNTS for token in found):
   557	        raise TemplateInvalid("report template contains an unknown gate token")
   558	    for token, count in TOKEN_COUNTS.items():
   559	        actual_count = found.count(token)
   560	        if ((token in OPTIONAL_TOKENS and actual_count not in (0, count))
   561	                or (token not in OPTIONAL_TOKENS and actual_count != count)):
   562	            raise TemplateInvalid(f"report template token count is invalid for {token}")
   563	    refused = verdict == "REFUSED"
   564	    values = {
   565	        "{{GATE_VERDICT}}": verdict,
   566	        "{{GATE_REASON}}": safe_json(reason if refused else "n/a"),
   567	        "{{GATE_COUNTS}}": safe_json("n/a" if refused else counts),
   568	        "{{GATE_HISTORY_STATUS}}": safe_json("n/a" if refused else history_status),
   569	        "{{GATE_WARNINGS}}": safe_json(warnings or []),
   570	        "{{GATE_SIBLING_SCAN}}": safe_json("n/a" if refused else sibling),
   571	        "{{GATE_ANCHOR_WRITTEN}}": "true" if anchor_written else "false",
   572	        "{{GATE_REPORT_DATE}}": report_date,
   573	    }
   574	    rendered = TOKEN_RE.sub(lambda match: values[match.group(0)], template)
   575	    raw = rendered.encode("utf-8")
   576	    if len(raw) > MAX_REPORT_BYTES:
   577	        raise TemplateInvalid("rendered report exceeds 4 MiB")
   578	    return raw
   579	
   580	
   581	def write_all(fd, raw):
   582	    offset = 0
   583	    while offset < len(raw):
   584	        written = os.write(fd, raw[offset:])
   585	        if written <= 0:
   586	            raise OSError("short write")
   587	        offset += written
   588	
   589	
   590	def fsync_directory_fd(fd):
   591	    os.fsync(fd)
   592	
   593	
   594	def publish_report(repo, run_dir, rule, raw):
   595	    fd, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".report-publication.", dir=run_dir)
   596	    linked = None
   597	    linked_parent_fd = None
   598	    cleanup_error = None
   599	    durability_unknown = False
   600	    try:
   601	        os.fchmod(fd, 0o644)
   602	        write_all(fd, raw)
   603	        os.fsync(fd)
   604	        os.close(fd)
   605	        fd = None
   606	        number = None
   607	        while True:
   608	            candidate = (rule["base"] if number is None else
   609	                         rule["suffixPrefix"] + f"_{number:02d}" + rule["suffixSuffix"])
   610	            candidate = validate_repo_path(repo, candidate, must_exist=False)
   611	            parent_rel = os.path.dirname(candidate)
   612	            parent = repo if not parent_rel else os.path.join(repo, parent_rel)
   613	            os.makedirs(parent, exist_ok=True)
   614	            if parent_rel:
   615	                validate_repo_path(repo, parent_rel, regular_file=False)
   616	            candidate_parent_fd = os.open(
   617	                parent, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW)
   618	            try:
   619	                os.link(temporary, os.path.basename(candidate),
   620	                        dst_dir_fd=candidate_parent_fd, follow_symlinks=False)
   621	                linked = candidate
   622	                linked_parent_fd = candidate_parent_fd
   623	                candidate_parent_fd = None
   624	                break
   625	            except FileExistsError:
   626	                number = rule["suffixStart"] if number is None else number + 1
   627	            finally:
   628	                if candidate_parent_fd is not None:
   629	                    os.close(candidate_parent_fd)
   630	        try:
   631	            fsync_directory_fd(linked_parent_fd)
   632	        except OSError:
   633	            durability_unknown = True
   634	    finally:
   635	        if linked_parent_fd is not None:
   636	            os.close(linked_parent_fd)
   637	        if fd is not None:
   638	            os.close(fd)
   639	        try:
   640	            os.unlink(temporary)
   641	        except FileNotFoundError:
   642	            pass
   643	        except OSError as exc:
   644	            cleanup_error = exc
   645	    return linked, durability_unknown, cleanup_error
   646	
   647	
   648	def report_status_update(last_run_path, state):
   649	    atomic(last_run_path, state)

   652	def release_lock(lock_path, lock_inode):
   653	    if os.stat(lock_path, follow_symlinks=False).st_ino != lock_inode:
   654	        raise OSError("lock path no longer names the owned inode")
   655	    os.unlink(lock_path)

   658	def rewrite_lock_holder(fd, holder):
   659	    raw = json_bytes(holder)
   660	    os.lseek(fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
   661	    os.ftruncate(fd, 0)
   662	    write_all(fd, raw)
   663	    os.fsync(fd)

   666	def quarantine_history(history_path, runid):
   667	    if not os.path.exists(history_path):
   668	        return
   669	    target = history_path + ".tainted-" + runid
   670	    try:
   671	        os.replace(history_path, target)
   672	    except OSError as exc:
   673	        raise HistoryQuarantineFailed("history-quarantine-failed") from exc
   674	    if os.path.exists(history_path):
   675	        raise HistoryQuarantineFailed("history-quarantine-failed")

   678	def taint_repo_from_run_dir(run_dir):
   679	    return os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(run_dir))))

   682	def early_taint(args):
   683	    run_dir = os.path.realpath(args.run_dir)
   684	    run_base = os.path.dirname(run_dir)
   685	    lock_path = os.path.join(run_base, "lock")
   686	    repo = os.path.realpath(args.repo_root) if args.repo_root else taint_repo_from_run_dir(run_dir)
   687	    state_dir = os.path.join(repo, ".claude", "state")
   688	    history_path = os.path.join(state_dir, "docaudit-history.json")
   689	    last_run_path = os.path.join(state_dir, "docaudit-last-run.json")
   690	    lock_fd = None
   691	    try:
   692	        expected = json.loads(args.expect_json)
   693	        validate_taint_evidence(expected)
   694	        if (expected.get("runid") != args.runid
   695	                or os.path.realpath(str(expected.get("runDir"))) != run_dir
   696	                or os.path.basename(run_dir) != args.runid):
   697	            raise Refused("EVIDENCE run identity mismatch")
   698	        lock_fd = os.open(lock_path, os.O_RDWR | os.O_NOFOLLOW)
   699	        try:
   700	            fcntl.flock(lock_fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
   701	        except BlockingIOError as exc:
   702	            raise Refused("lock is held by another process") from exc
   703	        lock_inode = os.fstat(lock_fd).st_ino
   704	        path_inode = os.stat(lock_path, follow_symlinks=False).st_ino
   705	        os.lseek(lock_fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
   706	        holder = parse_json(os.read(lock_fd, 65536), "lock")
   707	        if (not isinstance(holder, dict) or holder.get("runid") != args.runid
   708	                or path_inode != lock_inode or lock_inode != expected.get("lockIno")):
   709	            raise Refused("lock inode or runid mismatch")
   710	        if args.detail is not None:
   711	            if (len(args.detail) > 200 or any(ord(char) < 32 or ord(char) == 127
   712	                                              for char in args.detail)):
   713	                raise Refused("detail is invalid")
   714	        now = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()
   715	        last_state = {"runid": args.runid, "verdict": "REFUSED",
   716	                      "reason": "config-changed" if args.taint_observed == "config"
   717	                      else "history-changed",
   718	                      "observedBy": args.observed_by, "ts": now,
   719	                      "reportStatus": "not-requested"}
   720	        if args.detail is not None:

   749	                      else "quarantine-marker-unpersisted")

   751	                print(reason, file=sys.stderr)

   753	                              "reason": reason, "observedBy": args.observed_by}, sort_keys=True))

   756	        release_lock(lock_path, lock_inode)

   758	                          "reason": "history-changed", "observedBy": args.observed_by},
   759	                         sort_keys=True))
   760	        return 3
   761	    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, Refused, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   762	        print(json.dumps({"verdict": "REFUSED", "anchorWritten": False,
   763	                          "reason": str(exc)}, sort_keys=True))
   764	        return 3
   765	    finally:
   766	        if lock_fd is not None:
   767	            os.close(lock_fd)

   769	
   770	def add_warning(warnings, code):
   771	    if code not in warnings:
   772	        warnings.append(code)
   773	
   774	
   775	def finalize_report(repo, run_dir, rule, report_date, last_run_path, base_state, warnings,
   776	                    verdict, *, reason=None, counts=None, history_status=None,
   777	                    sibling=None, anchor_written=False):
   778	    if rule is None:
   779	        return None, base_state["reportStatus"]
   780	    report_path = None
   781	    status = "failed"
   782	    error_code = None
   783	    try:
   784	        template = load_report_template(run_dir)
   785	        rendered = render_report(
   786	            template, verdict, report_date, reason=reason, counts=counts,
   787	            history_status=history_status, warnings=warnings, sibling=sibling,
   788	            anchor_written=anchor_written)
   789	        report_path, durability_unknown, cleanup_error = publish_report(
   790	            repo, run_dir, rule, rendered)
   791	        if durability_unknown:
   792	            status = "written-durability-unknown"
   793	            add_warning(warnings, "reportDurabilityUnknown")
   794	        else:
   795	            status = "written"
   796	        if cleanup_error is not None:
   797	            add_warning(warnings, "reportWriteError")
   798	    except TemplateMissing:
   799	        error_code = "reportTemplateMissing"
   800	        add_warning(warnings, error_code)
   801	    except TemplateInvalid:
   802	        error_code = "reportTemplateInvalid"
   803	        add_warning(warnings, error_code)
   804	    except (OSError, ValueError):
   805	        error_code = "reportWriteError"
   806	        add_warning(warnings, error_code)
   807	    final_state = dict(base_state, reportStatus=status)
   808	    if report_path is not None:
   809	        final_state["reportPath"] = report_path
   810	    if error_code is not None:
   811	        final_state["reportError"] = error_code
   812	    try:
   813	        report_status_update(last_run_path, final_state)
   814	        persisted_status = status
   815	    except OSError:
   816	        add_warning(warnings, "reportStatusUpdateFailed")
   817	        persisted_status = base_state["reportStatus"]
   818	    return report_path, persisted_status
