   821	def main():
   822	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
   823	    parser.add_argument("--run-dir", required=True)
   824	    parser.add_argument("--repo-root")
   825	    parser.add_argument("--config")
   826	    parser.add_argument("--anchor-path")
   827	    parser.add_argument("--runid", required=True)
   828	    parser.add_argument("--expect-json", required=True)
   829	    parser.add_argument("--date")
   830	    parser.add_argument("--taint-observed", choices=("config", "history"))
   831	    parser.add_argument("--observed-by", choices=OBSERVERS)
   832	    parser.add_argument("--detail")
   833	    args = parser.parse_args()
   834	    if args.taint_observed:
   835	        if not args.observed_by:
   836	            parser.error("--observed-by is required with --taint-observed")
   837	        return early_taint(args)
   838	    if args.observed_by or args.detail:
   839	        parser.error("--observed-by/--detail require --taint-observed")
   840	    if not args.repo_root or not args.config or not args.anchor_path:
   841	        parser.error("--repo-root, --config, and --anchor-path are required")
   842	    repo_input = os.path.abspath(args.repo_root)
   843	    repo = os.path.realpath(repo_input)
   844	    run_dir = os.path.realpath(args.run_dir)
   845	    run_base = os.path.dirname(run_dir)
   846	    lock_path = os.path.join(run_base, "lock")
   847	    state_dir = os.path.join(repo, ".claude", "state")
   848	    history_path = os.path.join(state_dir, "docaudit-history.json")
   849	    last_run_path = os.path.join(state_dir, "docaudit-last-run.json")
   850	    config_path = os.path.abspath(args.config)
   851	    anchor_absolute = (args.anchor_path if os.path.isabs(args.anchor_path)
   852	                       else os.path.join(repo, args.anchor_path))
   853	    codex_review_status = {"state": None, "required": False, "degraded": False}
   854	
   855	    def relative_to_repo(path):
   856	        absolute = os.path.abspath(path)
   857	        try:
   858	            if os.path.commonpath([repo_input, absolute]) == repo_input:
   859	                return os.path.relpath(absolute, repo_input).replace(os.sep, "/")
   860	        except ValueError:
   861	            pass
   862	        return os.path.relpath(os.path.realpath(absolute), repo).replace(os.sep, "/")
   863	
   864	    try:
   865	        expected_run_dir = os.path.join(repo, ".claude", "state", "docaudit-run", args.runid)
   866	        run_rel = relative_to_repo(args.run_dir)
   867	        validate_repo_path(repo, run_rel, regular_file=False)
   868	        if run_dir != os.path.realpath(expected_run_dir):
   869	            raise ValueError("RUN_DIR is outside the run ledger")
   870	        config_rel = relative_to_repo(config_path)
   871	        config_path = os.path.join(repo, validate_repo_path(repo, config_rel))
   872	        anchor_rel = relative_to_repo(anchor_absolute)
   873	        anchor_path = os.path.join(
   874	            repo, validate_repo_path(repo, anchor_rel, must_exist=False, regular_file=False))
   875	        if os.path.lexists(anchor_path) and not os.path.isfile(anchor_path):
   876	            raise ValueError("anchor must be a regular file when present")
   877	    except ValueError as exc:
   878	        print(json.dumps({"verdict": "REFUSED", "anchorWritten": False,
   879	                          "reason": f"unsafe state path: {exc}",
   880	                          "codexReview": codex_review_status}, sort_keys=True))
   881	        return 3
   882	    lock_fd = None
   883	    owned = False
   884	    history_taint = False
   885	    anchor_taint = False
   886	    config_taint = False
   887	    expected = {}
   888	    lock_inode = None
   889	    holder = {}
   890	    history_signature = None
   891	    anchor_signature = None
   892	    config_signature = None
   893	    identity_ok = False
   894	    report_trusted = False
   895	    report_rule = None
   896	    warnings = []
   897	    try:
   898	        expected = json.loads(args.expect_json)
   899	        validate_evidence(expected)
   900	        if expected.get("runid") != args.runid or os.path.realpath(str(expected.get("runDir"))) != run_dir:
   901	            raise Refused("EVIDENCE run identity mismatch")
   902	        lock_fd = os.open(lock_path, os.O_RDWR | os.O_NOFOLLOW)
   903	        try:
   904	            fcntl.flock(lock_fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
   905	        except BlockingIOError as exc:
   906	            raise Refused("lock is held by another process") from exc
   907	        lock_inode = os.fstat(lock_fd).st_ino
   908	        try:
   909	            path_inode = os.stat(lock_path, follow_symlinks=False).st_ino
   910	        except OSError as exc:
   911	            raise Refused(f"lock path cannot be inspected: {exc}") from exc
   912	        os.lseek(lock_fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
   913	        holder = parse_json(os.read(lock_fd, 65536), "lock")
   914	        owned = (holder.get("runid") == args.runid
   915	                 and path_inode == lock_inode
   916	                 and lock_inode == expected["lockIno"])
   917	        if path_inode != lock_inode:
   918	            raise Refused("lock was unlinked/recreated")
   919	
   920	        # Every evidence file is read exactly once into this immutable snapshot.
   921	        manifest_raw = read_once(os.path.join(run_dir, "manifest.json"), "manifest.json")
   922	        dispatch_raw = read_once(os.path.join(run_dir, "dispatch.json"), "dispatch.json")
   923	        try:
   924	            impact_raw = read_once(os.path.join(run_dir, "impact.json"), "impact.json")
   925	        except Refused as exc:
   926	            raise Refused("impact sha mismatch") from exc
   927	        returns_raw = read_once(os.path.join(run_dir, "returns.json"), "returns.json")
   928	        manifest = parse_json(manifest_raw, "manifest.json")
   929	        if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
   930	            raise Refused("manifest must be an object")
   931	        verify_sha(manifest_raw, expected["manifest"], "manifest")
   932	        if manifest.get("sealed") is not True:
   933	            raise Refused("manifest is not sealed")
   934	        identity_mismatch = (holder.get("runid") != args.runid
   935	                             or manifest.get("runid") != args.runid
   936	                             or os.path.basename(run_dir) != args.runid
   937	                             or lock_inode != expected.get("lockIno"))
   938	        identity_ok = not identity_mismatch
   939	        try:
   940	            config_raw, config, config_signature = load_sealed_config(
   941	                config_path, expected["config"], with_signature=True)
   942	        except SealedConfigMismatch as exc:
   943	            config_taint = True
   944	            raise Refused(str(exc)) from exc
   945	        report_rule = validate_report_rule(manifest, repo, args.runid)
   946	        verify_sha(dispatch_raw, expected["dispatch"], "dispatch")
   947	        verify_sha(returns_raw, expected["returns"], "returns")
   948	        dispatch_doc = parse_json(dispatch_raw, "dispatch.json")
   949	        if not isinstance(dispatch_doc, dict) or sha256_bytes(impact_raw) != dispatch_doc.get("impactSha"):
   950	            raise Refused("impact sha mismatch")
   951	        impact_doc = parse_json(impact_raw, "impact.json")
   952	        returns = parse_json(returns_raw, "returns.json")
   953	        if not all(isinstance(value, dict) for value in (manifest, dispatch_doc, config)):
   954	            raise Refused("manifest, dispatch, and config must be objects")
   955	        codex_review_config = config.get("codexReview", {})
   956	        if not isinstance(codex_review_config, dict):
   957	            codex_review_config = {}
   958	        codex_review_required = codex_review_config.get("required", False)
   959	        if not isinstance(codex_review_required, bool):
   960	            raise Refused("codexReview.required must be boolean")
   961	        codex_review_status["required"] = codex_review_required
   962	        if codex_review_required and codex_review_config.get("enabled") is False:
   963	            raise Refused("codexReview.required conflicts with enabled:false")
   964	        backend = manifest.get("phase3Backend")
   965	        if (not isinstance(backend, str) or backend not in VALID_BACKENDS
   966	                or backend != config.get("phase3Backend", "workflow")):
   967	            raise Refused("sealed phase3Backend is invalid or does not match config")
   968	        report_trusted = identity_ok
   969	        validate_returns(returns)
   970	        if expected.get("attempt") != max((item["attempt"] for item in returns), default=0):
   971	            raise Refused("EVIDENCE attempt does not match returns")
   972	        history_entries = []
   973	        phase4_runs = []
   974	        history_raw = None
   975	        if expected["history"] == "none":
   976	            if os.path.exists(history_path) or expected.get("historyStatus") != "absent":
   977	                history_taint = os.path.exists(history_path)
   978	                raise Refused("history=none sentinel is invalid")
   979	        else:
   980	            history_raw, history_signature = read_state_once(history_path, "history")
   981	            if sha256_bytes(history_raw) != expected["history"]:
   982	                history_taint = True
   983	                raise Refused("history sha mismatch")
   984	            if expected.get("historyStatus") == "ok":
   985	                try:
   986	                    history_entries, phase4_runs, history_warnings = parse_history_document(
   987	                        parse_json(history_raw, "history"))
   988	                    for warning in history_warnings:
   989	                        add_warning(warnings, warning)
   990	                except (ValueError, Refused) as exc:
   991	                    history_taint = True
   992	                    raise Refused(f"history is corrupt: {exc}") from exc
   993	            elif expected.get("historyStatus") == "corrupt":
   994	                history_taint = True
   995	            else:
   996	                raise Refused("historyStatus is invalid")
   997	
   998	        if expected["anchor"] == "none":
   999	            if os.path.exists(anchor_path):
  1000	                anchor_taint = True
  1001	                raise Refused("anchor=none sentinel is invalid")
  1002	        else:
  1003	            anchor_raw, anchor_signature = read_state_once(anchor_path, "anchor")
  1004	            if sha256_bytes(anchor_raw) != expected["anchor"]:
  1005	                anchor_taint = True
  1006	                raise Refused("anchor sha mismatch")
  1007	
  1008	        if identity_mismatch:
  1009	            raise Refused("lock/manifest/run directory identity mismatch")
  1010	
  1011	        preflight = None
  1012	        preflight_path = os.path.join(run_dir, "preflight.json")
  1013	        if expected["preflight"] == "none":
  1014	            if manifest.get("preflightRequired") or os.path.exists(preflight_path):

  1069	        dispatched = manifest.get("dispatch")
  1070	        cached = manifest.get("cached")
  1071	        if not all(isinstance(value, list) for value in (impacted, dispatched, cached)):
  1072	            raise Refused("manifest path sets are invalid")

  1075	            raise Refused("full mode requires impacted documents unless the corpus is empty")

  1081	            if dispatch_doc.get(field) != manifest.get(field) and field not in {"historyStatus"}:
  1082	                raise Refused(f"manifest and dispatch.json differ at {field}")
  1083	        if dispatch_doc.get("historyStatus") != expected.get("historyStatus"):
  1084	            raise Refused("dispatch historyStatus mismatch")

  1090	            if not name.endswith(".json"):

  1127	        if codex_review_status["degraded"]:

  1144	                    or record.get("historyRunids") != runids):

  1148	        if preflight is not None:

  1150	        if phase4 is not None:

  1152	        verdict = "NEEDS_FIX" if has_fail else "CONSISTENT"
  1153	
  1154	        # The sibling scan remains inside the lock and precedes the indivisible barrier.
  1155	        sibling = run_sibling_step(manifest, returns, phase4, config, repo)
  1156	
  1157	        # Recheck every mutable root as one barrier immediately before state commit.
  1158	        lock_recheck(lock_fd, lock_path, args.runid, expected["lockIno"])
  1159	        head2 = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD"], capture_output=True,
  1160	                               text=True, check=True).stdout.strip()
  1161	        if head2 != head or run_tree_digest(repo, manifest) != digest:
  1162	            verify_audit_scope_at_barrier(repo, config, manifest.get("auditScopeSha"))
  1163	            raise Refused("HEAD/worktree changed before state write")
  1164	        if not state_unchanged(history_path, history_signature):
  1165	            history_taint = True
  1166	            raise Refused("history changed before state write")
  1167	        if not state_unchanged(anchor_path, anchor_signature):
  1168	            anchor_taint = True
  1169	            raise Refused("anchor changed before state write")
  1170	        if not state_unchanged(config_path, config_signature):
  1171	            config_taint = True
  1172	            raise Refused("config changed before state write")
  1173	        verify_audit_scope_at_barrier(repo, config, manifest.get("auditScopeSha"))
  1174	        if history_taint and os.path.exists(history_path):
  1175	            quarantine_history(history_path, args.runid)
  1176	            history_entries = []
  1177	            phase4_runs = []
  1178	            history_taint = False
  1179	        now = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()
  1180	        existing_keys = {(item["runid"], item["path"]) for item in history_entries}
  1181	        additions = []
  1182	        for path in impacted:
  1183	            if (args.runid, path) in existing_keys:
  1184	                raise Refused("history would duplicate (runid,path)")
  1185	            additions.append({"runid": args.runid, "path": path,
  1186	                              "contentSha": content_sha(repo, path),
  1187	                              "changeSetSha": manifest["changeSetSha"],
  1188	                              "contractVersion": manifest["contractVersion"],
  1189	                              "backend": backend,
  1190	                              "verdict": verdicts[path]["verdict"], "ts": now})
  1191	        flips, same_change_set_flips = verdict_flip_counts(
  1192	            history_entries, additions, dispatched)
  1193	        if flips:
  1194	            add_warning(
  1195	                warnings,
  1196	                f'verdict instability: {flips} document(s) changed verdict with unchanged '
  1197	                f'content since the previous run ({same_change_set_flips} with an unchanged '
  1198	                'change set) — single-pass verification samples the defect pool; "fix these '
  1199	                f'{flips} and re-run" is not guaranteed to converge (see ADOPTION)')
  1200	        phase4_flips = 0
  1201	        next_phase4_runs = list(phase4_runs)
  1202	        if phase4_eligible:
  1203	            truncated = len(phase4_findings) > 500
  1204	            current_phase4 = {
  1205	                "runid": args.runid,
  1206	                "ts": now,
  1207	                "worktreeDigest": manifest["worktreeDigest"],
  1208	                "contractVersion": manifest["contractVersion"],

  1211	                "unresolvedFileCount": phase4_unresolved,

  1217	            if truncated:
  1218	                add_warning(warnings, "Phase-4 findings were truncated; flip comparison skipped")
  1219	            else:
  1220	                phase4_flips = phase4_flip_count(phase4_runs, current_phase4)
  1221	            if phase4_flips:
  1222	                add_warning(
  1223	                    warnings,
  1224	                    f'Phase-4 instability: {phase4_flips} file(s) changed blocking status with '
  1225	                    'unchanged worktree, contract, config, and carry-forward inputs — the '
  1226	                    'codex full review samples '
  1227	                    'the defect pool; "fix N and re-run" is not guaranteed to converge '

  1231	                             "phase4Runs": candidate_runs}
  1232	            try:
  1233	                _entries_check, _runs_check, roundtrip_warnings = parse_history_document(
  1234	                    json.loads(json.dumps(candidate_doc, ensure_ascii=True)))
  1235	                if roundtrip_warnings:
  1236	                    raise ValueError("phase4Runs round-trip degraded")
  1237	                next_phase4_runs = candidate_runs
  1238	            except ValueError:
  1239	                add_warning(warnings, "Phase-4 history record failed round-trip validation")
  1240	                phase4_flips = 0
  1241	        atomic(history_path, {"entries": trim_history(history_entries + additions),
  1242	                              "phase4Runs": next_phase4_runs})
  1243	        report_status = "pending" if report_rule is not None else "not-requested"
  1244	        last_state = {"runid": args.runid, "verdict": verdict, "ts": now,
  1245	                      "reportStatus": report_status}
  1246	        atomic(last_run_path, last_state)
  1247	        anchor_written = False
  1248	        if verdict == "CONSISTENT":
  1249	            atomic(anchor_path, {"sha": head, "head": head, "digest": digest,
  1250	                                 "runid": args.runid,
  1251	                                 "contractVersion": manifest["contractVersion"]})
  1252	            anchor_written = True
  1253	        counts = {"impacted": len(impacted), "dispatch": len(dispatched),
  1254	                  "cached": len(cached),
  1255	                  "verdictFlipsUnchangedContent": flips,
  1256	                  "verdictFlipsUnchangedContentSameChangeSet": same_change_set_flips,
  1257	                  "phase4FlipsUnchangedContent": phase4_flips}
  1258	        report_verdict = verdict
  1259	        if verdict == "CONSISTENT" and codex_review_status["degraded"]:
  1260	            report_verdict = f"CONSISTENT (codex-review did not run: {codex_review_state})"
  1261	        report_path, report_status = finalize_report(
  1262	            repo, run_dir, report_rule, manifest["reportDate"], last_run_path,
  1263	            last_state, warnings, report_verdict, counts=counts,
  1264	            history_status=expected["historyStatus"], sibling=sibling,
  1265	            anchor_written=anchor_written)
  1266	        try:
  1267	            release_lock(lock_path, lock_inode)
  1268	        except OSError:
  1269	            add_warning(warnings, "lockReleaseFailed")
  1270	        os.close(lock_fd)
  1271	        lock_fd = None
  1272	        result = {"verdict": verdict, "anchorWritten": anchor_written,
  1273	                  "runid": args.runid, "counts": counts,
  1274	                  "historyStatus": expected["historyStatus"], "warnings": warnings,
  1275	                  "siblingScan": sibling, "reportStatus": report_status,
  1276	                  "codexReview": codex_review_status}
  1277	        if report_path is not None:
  1278	            result["reportPath"] = report_path
  1279	        print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True))
  1280	        return 0
  1281	    except (Refused, HistoryQuarantineFailed, OSError, ValueError, KeyError, json.JSONDecodeError,
  1282	            subprocess.CalledProcessError) as exc:
  1283	        reason = str(exc)
  1284	        # Degrade only state owned by this run; never touch a later run's state.
  1285	        report_path = None
  1286	        report_status = None
  1287	        if owned:
  1288	            quarantine_failed = False
  1289	            try:
  1290	                if history_taint and os.path.exists(history_path):
  1291	                    quarantine_history(history_path, args.runid)
  1292	                    history_taint = False
  1293	            except HistoryQuarantineFailed:
  1294	                quarantine_failed = True
  1295	            try:
  1296	                if anchor_taint and os.path.exists(anchor_path):
  1297	                    os.unlink(anchor_path)
  1298	            except OSError:
  1299	                pass
  1300	            can_report = (report_trusted and report_rule is not None and not config_taint
  1301	                          and not quarantine_failed)
  1302	            state_written = False
  1303	            holder_marker_written = False
  1304	            if identity_ok:
  1305	                now = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()
  1306	                recorded_reason = ("history-quarantine-failed" if quarantine_failed
  1307	                                   else "config-changed" if config_taint else reason)
  1308	                last_state = {"runid": args.runid, "verdict": "REFUSED",
  1309	                              "reason": recorded_reason, "ts": now,
  1310	                              "reportStatus": "pending" if can_report else "not-requested"}
  1311	                if config_taint:
  1312	                    last_state["expectedConfigSha"] = expected.get("config")
  1313	                    last_state["configAcceptanceRequired"] = True
  1314	                if quarantine_failed:
  1315	                    last_state["historyQuarantineFailed"] = True
  1316	                try:
  1317	                    atomic(last_run_path, last_state)
  1318	                    state_written = True
  1319	                    report_status = last_state["reportStatus"]
  1320	                except OSError:
  1321	                    add_warning(warnings, "reportStatusUpdateFailed")
  1322	                if quarantine_failed and lock_fd is not None:
  1323	                    try:
  1324	                        rewrite_lock_holder(
  1325	                            lock_fd, dict(holder, historyQuarantineFailed=True))
  1326	                        holder_marker_written = True
  1327	                    except OSError:
  1328	                        pass
  1329	                    if not state_written and not holder_marker_written:
  1330	                        reason = "quarantine-marker-unpersisted"
  1331	                        print(reason, file=sys.stderr)
  1332	                if can_report and state_written:
  1333	                    report_path, report_status = finalize_report(
  1334	                        repo, run_dir, report_rule, manifest["reportDate"], last_run_path,
  1335	                        last_state, warnings, "REFUSED", reason=reason,
  1336	                        anchor_written=False)
  1337	            if lock_fd is not None and not quarantine_failed:
  1338	                try:
  1339	                    release_lock(lock_path, lock_inode)
  1340	                except OSError:
  1341	                    add_warning(warnings, "lockReleaseFailed")

  1343	        result = {"verdict": "REFUSED", "anchorWritten": False, "reason": output_reason,

  1347	        if report_status is not None:
  1348	            result["reportStatus"] = report_status
  1349	        if report_path is not None:
  1350	            result["reportPath"] = report_path
  1351	        print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True))
  1352	        return 3
  1353	    finally:
  1354	        if lock_fd is not None:
  1355	            os.close(lock_fd)

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Validate and atomically write orchestrator-owned evidence."""

     4	import argparse

    12	VALID_VERDICTS = {"PASS", "WARN", "FAIL"}
    13	
    14	
    15	def validate_returns(value):
    16	    if not isinstance(value, list):
    17	        raise ValueError("returns must be an array")
    18	    seen = set()
    19	    for index, item in enumerate(value):
    20	        if not isinstance(item, dict):
    21	            raise ValueError(f"returns[{index}] must be an object")
    22	        attempt = item.get("attempt")
    23	        if isinstance(attempt, bool) or not isinstance(attempt, int) or not 1 <= attempt <= 3:
    24	            raise ValueError(f"returns[{index}].attempt must be 1..3")
    25	        if not isinstance(item.get("assignedPath"), str):
    26	            raise ValueError(f"returns[{index}].assignedPath is required")
    27	        for field in ("returnedPath", "rationale", "suggestion"):
    28	            if item.get(field) is not None and not isinstance(item.get(field), str):
    29	                raise ValueError(f"returns[{index}].{field} must be string or null")
    30	        if item.get("verdict") is not None and item.get("verdict") not in VALID_VERDICTS:
    31	            raise ValueError(f"returns[{index}].verdict is invalid")
    32	        key = (attempt, item["assignedPath"])
    33	        if key in seen:
    34	            raise ValueError("duplicate (attempt,assignedPath)")
    35	        seen.add(key)
    36	
    37	
    38	def validate(name, value):
    39	    if name == "returns":
    40	        validate_returns(value)
    41	        return
    42	    if not isinstance(value, dict):
    43	        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be an object")
    44	    if name == "preflight":
    45	        required = {"state": str, "findings": list, "userDecision": (str, type(None)), "parsed": bool}
    46	        for key, typ in required.items():
    47	            if not isinstance(value.get(key), typ):
    48	                raise ValueError(f"preflight.{key} is invalid")
    49	    elif name == "phase4" and not isinstance(value.get("findings", []), list):
    50	        raise ValueError("phase4.findings must be an array")
    51	
    52	
    53	def main():
    54	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    55	    parser.add_argument("--run-dir", required=True)
    56	    parser.add_argument("--name", required=True, choices=["preflight", "returns", "phase4"])
    57	    parser.add_argument("--stdin", action="store_true")
    58	    parser.add_argument("--evidence", required=True)
    59	    args = parser.parse_args()
    60	    if not args.stdin:
    61	        parser.error("--stdin is required")
    62	    try:
    63	        evidence = json.loads(args.evidence)
    64	        if not isinstance(evidence, dict):
    65	            raise ValueError("--evidence must be an object")
    66	        if os.path.realpath(str(evidence.get("runDir"))) != os.path.realpath(args.run_dir):
    67	            raise ValueError("EVIDENCE runDir mismatch")
    68	        value = json.load(sys.stdin)
    69	        validate(args.name, value)
    70	        raw = (json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, indent=2) + "\n").encode("utf-8")
    71	        os.makedirs(args.run_dir, exist_ok=True)
    72	        fd, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=f".{args.name}.", dir=args.run_dir)
    73	        try:
    74	            with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as handle:
    75	                handle.write(raw)
    76	                handle.flush()
    77	                os.fsync(handle.fileno())
    78	            os.replace(temporary, os.path.join(args.run_dir, args.name + ".json"))
    79	        finally:
    80	            if os.path.exists(temporary):
    81	                os.unlink(temporary)
    82	        evidence[args.name] = "sha256:" + hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
    83	        if args.name == "returns":
    84	            evidence["attempt"] = max((item["attempt"] for item in value), default=0)
    85	    except (OSError, ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
    86	        print(f"write-evidence: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
    87	        return 2
    88	    print(json.dumps(evidence, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True))
    89	    return 0

    93	    sys.exit(main())

    10	def read(path):
    11	    with open(os.path.join(ROOT, path), encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as handle:
    12	        return handle.read()

    14	
    15	def normalize(text):
    16	    return " ".join(text.split())
    17	
    18	
    19	def markdown_units(text):
    20	    """Split Markdown at the smallest contract unit: row, list item, or paragraph."""
    21	    units = []
    22	    pending = []
    23	    pending_kind = None
    24	
    25	    def flush():
    26	        nonlocal pending, pending_kind
    27	        if pending:
    28	            units.append("\n".join(pending))
    29	        pending = []
    30	        pending_kind = None
    31	
    32	    table_row = re.compile(r"^\s*\|")
    33	    list_start = re.compile(r"^\s*(?:[-+*]|\d+[.)])\s+")
    34	    for line in text.splitlines():
    35	        if not line.strip():
    36	            flush()
    37	        elif table_row.match(line):
    38	            flush()
    39	            units.append(line)
    40	        elif list_start.match(line):
    41	            flush()
    42	            pending = [line]
    43	            pending_kind = "list"
    44	        elif pending_kind == "list":
    45	            pending.append(line)
    46	        else:
    47	            pending.append(line)
    48	            pending_kind = "paragraph"
    49	    flush()
    50	    return units
    51	
    52	
    53	SEAM_RE = re.compile(
    54	    r"(?<![A-Za-z0-9_])(?:webExtract|ax|codexReview|codex)(?![A-Za-z0-9_])",
    55	    re.IGNORECASE,
    56	)
    57	OLD_WORDING_RES = (
    58	    re.compile(r"\bconditional" + r"-force\b", re.IGNORECASE),
    59	    re.compile(r"\bauto" + r"-used\b", re.IGNORECASE),
    60	    re.compile("自動" + "使用"),
    61	    re.compile(
    62	        r"\ban absent key (?:still )?(?:remains enabled by default|defaults to enabled)\b",
    63	        re.IGNORECASE,
    64	    ),
    65	    re.compile("キーが無い場合は" + "従来どおり有効"),
    66	)
    67	HISTORY_HEADINGS = (
    68	    "**v0.14.0 behavior changes:**",
    69	    "**v0.14.0 の挙動変更:**",
    70	)
    71	
    72	
    73	def residue_hits(path, text):
    74	    hits = []
    75	    for number, unit in enumerate(markdown_units(text), 1):
    76	        if (path == "tests/test_v014_contracts.py"
    77	                and "def test_v014_behavior_changes_paragraph" in unit):
    78	            continue
    79	        stripped = unit.lstrip()
    80	        if stripped.startswith(HISTORY_HEADINGS):
    81	            continue
    82	        compact = normalize(unit)
    83	        if SEAM_RE.search(compact) and any(pattern.search(compact)
    84	                                           for pattern in OLD_WORDING_RES):
    85	            hits.append((path, number, compact))
    86	    return hits

   163	        self.assertIn("probe-error", resume)
   164	        self.assertNotIn(
   165	            "Phase 4 may restore any missing operational availability, reason, or binary variables from `rebind`",
   166	            resume,
   167	        )

   169	    def test_v015_behavior_change_paragraphs_are_exact(self):
   170	        expected = {
   171	            "docs/ADOPTION.md": (
   172	                "**v0.15.0 behavior changes:**",
   173	                (
   174	                    "webExtract and codexReview are now key-gated like symbolGraph/docGraph/semanticSearch: an absent key reports not-configured and never runs the tool — ax and codex no longer run implicitly on configs without those keys (previously an absent key defaulted to enabled); in v0.16.0 a directly invoked probe with an unreadable, absent, or omitted config exits 2 without JSON output instead of reporting invalid-config, while a sealed-config mismatch exits 7, and the codex probe collects no caller CODEX_HOME/auth.json information for a keyless valid config (neutral values are recorded)",
   175	                    "for a new run, or a run resumed before its codex review has run, a keyless config therefore loses the Phase-4 codex review and its verdict-affecting critical/high findings — an audit that was NEEDS FIX only because of implicit codex findings can become CONSISTENT after upgrading; add \"codexReview\": {} to keep the old best-effort behavior, or additionally \"required\": true for a separate, stronger fail-closed guarantee (a non-completed review becomes REFUSED — this is NOT the old implicit behavior)",
   176	                    "on resume, the operational webExtract and codexReview state is re-derived by re-running their key-gated probes against the current config (probe records are overwritten accordingly); a run whose codex review already completed keeps those findings — cross-version in-flight resume is discouraged: start a fresh run (a mechanical prohibition is tracked in #59)",
   177	                    "indexing and contextMode keep their enabled-by-default behavior (intentional: they reduce token consumption); enabled:false and invalid-config semantics are unchanged for all four seams, and bin validation is unchanged for the three bin-bearing seams (indexing/webExtract/codexReview; contextMode has no bin)",
   178	                ),
   179	            ),
   180	            "docs/ADOPTION.ja.md": (
   181	                "**v0.15.0 の挙動変更:**",
   182	                (
   183	                    "webExtract と codexReview は symbolGraph/docGraph/semanticSearch と同じ key-gated になった: キーが無い場合は not-configured と報告し、tool を一切起動しない — キー無し config で ax / codex が暗黙に実行されることはなくなった（従来はキー不在＝既定有効）。v0.16.0 では probe を単体で直接呼んだ場合、読めない・存在しない・省略された config は invalid-config JSON を返さず exit 2、封印不一致は exit 7 になる。また codex probe はキー無しの有効な config では呼び出し元の CODEX_HOME / auth.json 情報を収集しない（中立値を記録する）",
   184	                    "したがって、新規 run および codex review 実行前に resume した run では、キー無し config は Phase-4 codex review と、その verdict に影響する critical/high 所見を失う — 暗黙の codex 所見だけが理由で NEEDS FIX だった audit は、更新後 CONSISTENT になり得る。旧来の best-effort 挙動を維持するには \"codexReview\": {} を追加する。さらに \"required\": true を付けると別種のより強い fail-closed 保証になる（未完走の review が REFUSED になる — これは旧来の暗黙挙動ではない）",
   185	                    "resume 時、webExtract と codexReview の運用状態は key-gated な probe を現在の config に対して再実行して導出し直す（probe 記録も対応して上書きされる）。codex review が既に完走した run はその所見を保持する — 版をまたぐ resume は非推奨であり、新しい run を開始すること（機械的な禁止機構は #59 で追跡）",
   186	                    "indexing と contextMode は従来どおり既定有効（トークン消費を減らす装置としての意図的設計）。enabled:false と invalid-config の意味論は 4 seam すべてで不変、bin 検査は bin を持つ 3 seam（indexing/webExtract/codexReview）で不変（contextMode に bin は無い）",
   187	                ),
   188	            ),
   189	        }
   190	        for path, (heading, sentences) in expected.items():
   191	            units = [normalize(unit) for unit in markdown_units(read(path))]
   192	            start = next(index for index, unit in enumerate(units)
   193	                         if unit.startswith(heading))
   194	            self.assertEqual(units[start:start + 4],
   195	                             [heading + " " + sentences[0], *sentences[1:]], path)

   214	    def test_code_review_behavior_is_unchanged_by_wording_update(self):
   215	        skill = normalize(read("skills/audit/SKILL.md"))
   216	        contracts = (
   217	            "If completion cannot be confirmed, do not invent findings and use `CODE_REVIEW_STATE=not-model-invocable`.",
   218	            "If execution reports the specific `disable-model-invocation` block, bind `CODE_REVIEW_STATE=not-model-invocable` without WARN.",
   219	            "💡 code-review: not run — the audit does not start /code-review itself yet (tracked in #66); run it when offered in an interactive audit, or before the audit, if you want this layer included. (expected)",
   220	            "In a non-interactive session, do not offer the question and use that expected state directly.",
   221	            "before the gate and only once, use AskUserQuestion to offer running the configured `/code-review` command.",
   222	        )
   223	        for contract in contracts:
   224	            with self.subTest(contract=contract):
   225	                self.assertGreaterEqual(skill.count(normalize(contract)), 1)

   227	    def test_minimum_unit_scanner_preserves_enabled_by_default_seams(self):
   228	        old = "conditional" + "-force"
   229	        fixture = (
   230	            "| contextMode | " + old + " |\n"
   231	            "| webExtract | " + old + " |\n\n"
   232	            "- contextMode stays " + old + "\n"
   233	            "  across this continuation\n"
   234	            "- codexReview was " + old + "\n\n"
   235	            "indexing stays " + old + ".\n\n"
   236	            "ax was " + old + ".\n"
   237	        )
   238	        hits = residue_hits("fixture.md", fixture)
   239	        self.assertEqual(len(hits), 3)
   240	        self.assertTrue(any("webExtract" in hit[2] for hit in hits))
   241	        self.assertTrue(any("codexReview" in hit[2] for hit in hits))
   242	        self.assertTrue(any("ax was" in hit[2] for hit in hits))
   243	
   244	        preserved_paths = {
   245	            "skills/audit/references/config-schema.md",
   246	            "skills/init/SKILL.md",
   247	            "docs/ADOPTION.md",
   248	            "docs/ADOPTION.ja.md",
   249	            "README.md",
   250	        }
   251	        witnessed = set()
   252	        for path in preserved_paths:
   253	            for unit in markdown_units(read(path)):
   254	                compact = normalize(unit)
   255	                if (re.search(r"indexing|contextMode|context-mode|mdq", compact,
   256	                              re.IGNORECASE)
   257	                        and any(pattern.search(compact)
   258	                                for pattern in OLD_WORDING_RES)):
   259	                    witnessed.add(path)
   260	                    self.assertEqual(residue_hits(path, unit), [], (path, compact))
   261	        self.assertEqual(witnessed, preserved_paths)
   262	
   263	    def test_no_nonhistorical_key_gate_residue_in_tracked_files(self):
   264	        proc = subprocess.run(
   265	            ["git", "ls-files"], cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
   266	        paths = [path for path in proc.stdout.splitlines()
   267	                 if path and not path.startswith("tasks/")]
   268	        scanned = set()
   269	        unit_count = 0
   270	        hits = []
   271	        for path in paths:
   272	            absolute = os.path.join(ROOT, path)
   273	            if not os.path.isfile(absolute):
   274	                continue
   275	            text = read(path)
   276	            scanned.add(path)
   277	            unit_count += len(markdown_units(text))
   278	            hits.extend(residue_hits(path, text))
   279	        representatives = {
   280	            "docs/ADOPTION.md", "skills/init/SKILL.md", "README.md",
   281	            "tests/test_v014_contracts.py",
   282	        }
   283	        self.assertGreater(unit_count, 0)
   284	        self.assertTrue(representatives.issubset(scanned))
   285	        print("v0.15 residue scan: "
   286	              f"files={len(scanned)} units={unit_count} hits={len(hits)}")
   287	        self.assertEqual(hits, [])

    85	def file_sha(path):
    86	    with open(path, "rb") as handle:
    87	        return "sha256:" + hashlib.sha256(handle.read()).hexdigest()
    88	
    89	
    90	def script(name):
    91	    return os.path.join(SCRIPTS, name)
    92	
    93	
    94	def run(name, *args, input_text=None, cwd=None, env=None):
    95	    command = (["bash", script(name)] if name.endswith(".sh")
    96	               else [sys.executable, script(name)])
    97	    return subprocess.run(command + list(args), input=input_text, capture_output=True,
    98	                          text=True, cwd=cwd, env=env)
    99	
   100	
   101	def load_module(name):
   102	    path = script(name)
   103	    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("v016_" + name.replace("-", "_"), path)
   104	    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
   105	    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
   106	    return module

   109	class TestV016Contracts(unittest.TestCase):
   110	    maxDiff = None

   112	    def test_ct_1_registry_equivalence(self):
   113	        with open(SKILL, encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   114	            skill = handle.read()
   115	
   116	        flag_files = set()
   117	        for name in os.listdir(SCRIPTS):
   118	            if not (name.endswith(".py") or name.endswith(".sh")):
   119	                continue
   120	            with open(script(name), encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   121	                source = handle.read()
   122	            if (re.search(r'add_argument\("--expect-config-sha"', source)
   123	                    or re.search(r'--expect-config-sha\)', source)):
   124	                flag_files.add(name)
   125	        expected_flags = {name for name, mode, _code, _token in CONSUMER_REGISTRY
   126	                          if mode in {"required", "conditional", "optional"}}
   127	        expected_flags.discard("check-docs.py")
   128	        expected_flags.add("import-audit-scope.py")
   129	        self.assertEqual(flag_files, expected_flags)
   130	
   131	        call_lines = [line for line in skill.splitlines()
   132	                      if ('--expect-config-sha "$CONFIG_SHA"' in line
   133	                          or '--expect-config-sha "$PRECHECK_CONFIG_SHA"' in line)]
   134	        cfg_lines = [line for line in skill.splitlines() if '"$CFG"' in line]
   135	        exemptions = [line for line in cfg_lines if "CONFIG_SHA" not in line]
   136	        self.assertEqual(len(call_lines), 22)
   137	        self.assertEqual(len(exemptions), 3)
   138	        self.assertIn("ANCHOR_PATH=", exemptions[0])
   139	        self.assertTrue(any("import-audit-scope.py" in line and "--check" in line
   140	                            for line in exemptions))
   141	        self.assertTrue(any("decide-verdict.py" in line for line in exemptions))
   142	        for line in cfg_lines:
   143	            classified = (line in exemptions
   144	                          or '--expect-config-sha "$CONFIG_SHA"' in line
   145	                          or ('sealed_config.py' in line
   146	                              and '--expect-sha "$CONFIG_SHA"' in line))
   147	            self.assertTrue(classified, line)
   148	
   149	        for key, variable, default, raw in GETTER_REGISTRY:
   150	            prefix = (f'{variable}="$(python3 "$SD/scripts/sealed_config.py" '
   151	                      f'--config "$CFG" --expect-sha "$CONFIG_SHA" --get {key} ')
   152	            matches = [line for line in skill.splitlines() if prefix in line]
   153	            self.assertEqual(len(matches), 1, (key, variable, matches))
   154	            rendered_default = ({'"workflow"': "'\"workflow\"'",
   155	                                 "{}": "'{}'", "[]": "'[]'"}.get(default, default))
   156	            self.assertIn("--default " + rendered_default,
   157	                          matches[0])
   158	            self.assertEqual("--raw" in matches[0], raw)
   159	            assignments = re.findall(rf'(?m)^.*\b{re.escape(variable)}=', skill)
   160	            self.assertEqual(len(assignments), 1, variable)
   161	
   162	        for name in SHELL_CONSUMERS:
   163	            with open(script(name), encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   164	                source = handle.read()
   165	            self.assertEqual(source.count("sealed_config.py"), 1, name)
   166	            self.assertNotIn("json.load(open", source, name)
   167	
   168	        with open(script("decide-verdict.py"), encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   169	            tree = ast.parse(handle.read())
   170	        observed = None
   171	        for node in tree.body:
   172	            if isinstance(node, ast.Assign) and any(
   173	                    isinstance(target, ast.Name) and target.id == "OBSERVERS"
   174	                    for target in node.targets):
   175	                observed = ast.literal_eval(node.value)
   176	        self.assertEqual(observed, OBSERVERS)
   177	        counts = (len(call_lines), len(exemptions), len(GETTER_REGISTRY),
   178	                  len(CONSUMER_REGISTRY), len(observed))
   179	        self.assertEqual(counts, (22, 3, 13, 21, 19))
   180	        print("call sites 22／exempt 3／getters 13／scripts 21／observers 19")
   181

   215	                                     "--baseline-ok", "true", "--history", history,
   216	                                     "--expect-history-sha", "none",

   222	    def test_ct_2_all_consumer_match_mismatch_pairs(self):
   223	        checked = set()
   224	        mismatch_contract = {
   225	            name: (code, token) for name, _mode, code, token in CONSUMER_REGISTRY
   226	        }
   227	        fx = RunFixture(self)

   393	    def test_ct_2b_sitecustomize_hooks_builtins_and_os_open(self):
   394	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
   395	            config = os.path.join(temp, "config.json")
   396	            log = os.path.join(temp, "opens.log")
   397	            write(config, "{}\n")
   398	            hook = textwrap.dedent(r'''
   399	                import builtins, json, os, sys
   400	                _builtins_open = builtins.open
   401	                _os_open = os.open
   402	                _target = os.environ.get("DOCAUDIT_COUNT_PATH")
   403	                _log = os.environ.get("DOCAUDIT_COUNT_LOG")
   404	                def _record(kind, path):
   405	                    try: same = os.path.realpath(os.fspath(path)) == os.path.realpath(_target)
   406	                    except Exception: same = False
   407	                    if same:
   408	                        with _builtins_open(_log, "a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   409	                            handle.write(json.dumps({"kind":kind,"argv":sys.argv}) + "\n")
   410	                def counted_open(path, *args, **kwargs):
   411	                    handle = _builtins_open(path, *args, **kwargs)
   412	                    _record("builtins.open", path)
   413	                    return handle
   414	                def counted_os_open(path, *args, **kwargs):
   415	                    fd = _os_open(path, *args, **kwargs)
   416	                    _record("os.open", path)
   417	                    return fd
   418	                builtins.open = counted_open
   419	                os.open = counted_os_open
   420	            ''')
   421	            write(os.path.join(temp, "sitecustomize.py"), hook)
   422	            env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONPATH=temp, DOCAUDIT_COUNT_PATH=config,
   423	                       DOCAUDIT_COUNT_LOG=log)
   424	            probe = subprocess.run([
   425	                sys.executable, "-c",
   426	                "import os; f=open(os.environ['DOCAUDIT_COUNT_PATH']); f.close(); "
   427	                "fd=os.open(os.environ['DOCAUDIT_COUNT_PATH'], os.O_RDONLY); os.close(fd)"],
   428	                capture_output=True, text=True, env=env)
   429	            self.assertEqual(probe.returncode, 0, probe.stderr)
   430	            sealed = run("sealed_config.py", "--config", config, "--expect-sha",
   431	                         file_sha(config), "--print", env=env)
   432	            self.assertEqual(sealed.returncode, 0, sealed.stderr)
   433	            with open(log, encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   434	                events = [json.loads(line) for line in handle]
   435	            self.assertIn("builtins.open", {item["kind"] for item in events})
   436	            self.assertIn("os.open", {item["kind"] for item in events})
   437	            sealed_events = [item for item in events if "sealed_config.py" in " ".join(item["argv"])]
   438	            self.assertEqual([item["kind"] for item in sealed_events], ["os.open"])
   439	            self.assertIn("builtins.open = counted_open", hook)
   440	            self.assertIn("os.open = counted_os_open", hook)
   441	
   442	    def test_ct_2b_all_consumer_read_counts_and_child_sha(self):
   443	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
   444	            log = os.path.join(temp, "opens.log")
   445	            hook = textwrap.dedent(r'''
   446	                import builtins, json, os, sys
   447	                _builtins_open = builtins.open
   448	                _os_open = os.open
   449	                _target = os.environ.get("DOCAUDIT_COUNT_PATH")
   450	                _log = os.environ.get("DOCAUDIT_COUNT_LOG")
   451	                _swap_for = os.environ.get("DOCAUDIT_SWAP_FOR")
   452	                _swap_bytes = os.environ.get("DOCAUDIT_SWAP_BYTES")
   453	                _swap_done = os.environ.get("DOCAUDIT_SWAP_DONE")
   454	                def _record(kind, path):
   455	                    try: same = os.path.realpath(os.fspath(path)) == os.path.realpath(_target)
   456	                    except Exception: same = False
   457	                    if not same:
   458	                        return
   459	                    with _builtins_open(_log, "a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   460	                        handle.write(json.dumps({"kind":kind,"argv":sys.argv}) + "\n")
   461	                    if (_swap_for and os.path.basename(sys.argv[0]) == _swap_for
   462	                            and _swap_bytes and _swap_done):
   463	                        try:
   464	                            marker = _os_open(_swap_done, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL, 0o600)
   465	                        except FileExistsError:
   466	                            return
   467	                        os.close(marker)
   468	                        replacement = _target + ".ct2b-swap"
   469	                        fd = _os_open(replacement, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC, 0o600)
   470	                        try: os.write(fd, _swap_bytes.encode("utf-8"))
   471	                        finally: os.close(fd)
   472	                        os.replace(replacement, _target)
   473	                def counted_open(path, *args, **kwargs):
   474	                    handle = _builtins_open(path, *args, **kwargs)
   475	                    _record("builtins.open", path)
   476	                    return handle
   477	                def counted_os_open(path, *args, **kwargs):
   478	                    fd = _os_open(path, *args, **kwargs)
   479	                    _record("os.open", path)
   480	                    return fd
   481	                builtins.open = counted_open
   482	                os.open = counted_os_open
   483	            ''')
   484	            write(os.path.join(temp, "sitecustomize.py"), hook)
   485	
   486	            def counted_env(config_path, **extra):
   487	                value = dict(os.environ, PYTHONPATH=temp,
   488	                             DOCAUDIT_COUNT_PATH=config_path,
   489	                             DOCAUDIT_COUNT_LOG=log)
   490	                value.update(extra)
   491	                return value
   492	
   493	            def execute(name, args, config_path, input_text="", env_extra=None):
   494	                write(log, "")
   495	                env = counted_env(config_path, **(env_extra or {}))
   496	                proc = run(name, *args, input_text=input_text, env=env)
   497	                with open(log, encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   498	                    events = [json.loads(line) for line in handle]
   499	                return proc, events
   500	
   501	            def assert_child_sha(events, expected_sha):
   502	                children = [item for item in events
   503	                            if os.path.basename(item["argv"][0]) == "change-set-sha.py"]
   504	                self.assertEqual(len(children), 1, events)
   505	                argv = children[0]["argv"]
   506	                position = argv.index("--expect-config-sha")
   507	                self.assertEqual(argv[position + 1], expected_sha)
   508	
   509	            fx = RunFixture(self)
   510	            impact_path = os.path.join(fx.repo, "impact-count.json")
   511	            write(impact_path, json.dumps({"impacted": []}) + "\n")
   512	            args_by_name = self._base_args(fx, impact_path)
   513	            direct = (*SHELL_CONSUMERS, "generic-layers.py", "fix-scope.py",
   514	                      "resolve-impact.py", "impact-supplement.py",
   515	                      "codex-review-plan.py", "change-set-sha.py")
   516	            measured = set()
   517	            for name in direct:
   518	                with self.subTest(name=name, kind="direct"):
   519	                    proc, events = execute(name, args_by_name[name], fx.config_path)
   520	                    self.assertEqual(proc.returncode, 0, proc.stdout + proc.stderr)

    85	def file_sha(path):
    86	    with open(path, "rb") as handle:
    87	        return "sha256:" + hashlib.sha256(handle.read()).hexdigest()
    88	
    89	
    90	def script(name):
    91	    return os.path.join(SCRIPTS, name)
    92	
    93	
    94	def run(name, *args, input_text=None, cwd=None, env=None):
    95	    command = (["bash", script(name)] if name.endswith(".sh")
    96	               else [sys.executable, script(name)])
    97	    return subprocess.run(command + list(args), input=input_text, capture_output=True,
    98	                          text=True, cwd=cwd, env=env)
    99	
   100	
   101	def load_module(name):
   102	    path = script(name)
   103	    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("v016_" + name.replace("-", "_"), path)
   104	    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
   105	    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
   106	    return module

   632	    def test_ct_3_toctou_taint_acceptance_flow(self):
   633	        fx = RunFixture(self)
   634	        self.assertEqual(fx.open().returncode, 0)
   635	        with open(fx.config_path, "rb") as handle:
   636	            original = handle.read()
   637	        with open(fx.config_path, "ab") as handle:
   638	            handle.write(b" ")
   639	        mismatch = run("mdq-index.sh", "--config", fx.config_path,
   640	                       "--expect-config-sha", fx.evidence["config"],
   641	                       "--repo-root", fx.repo)
   642	        self.assertEqual(mismatch.returncode, 7)
   643	        write(fx.config_path, original)
   644	        tainted = fx.call(
   645	            "decide-verdict.py", "--run-dir", fx.run_dir, "--repo-root", fx.repo,
   646	            "--runid", fx.runid, "--expect-json", json.dumps(fx.evidence),
   647	            "--taint-observed", "config", "--observed-by", "mdq-index.sh")
   648	        self.assertEqual(tainted.returncode, 3, tainted.stdout + tainted.stderr)
   649	        refused = fx.open(runid="20260818T120001Z-abcdef13")
   650	        self.assertEqual(refused.returncode, 6, refused.stdout + refused.stderr)
   651	        accepted = fx.open(runid="20260818T120001Z-abcdef13", accept=True)
   652	        self.assertEqual(accepted.returncode, 0, accepted.stdout + accepted.stderr)
   653	        released = fx.call("open-run.py", "--run-base", fx.run_base, "--repo-root", fx.repo,
   654	                           "--release", "--runid", fx.runid)
   655	        self.assertEqual(released.returncode, 0, released.stdout + released.stderr)
   656	        reopened = fx.open(runid="20260818T120002Z-abcdef14")
   657	        self.assertEqual(reopened.returncode, 0, reopened.stdout + reopened.stderr)
   658	
   659	        with open(SKILL, encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   660	            skill = handle.read()
   661	        stopping = skill.index("If any top-level consumer returns exit 7")
   662	        seal_release = skill.index("Any other non-zero exit, except exit 7")
   663	        self.assertLess(stopping, seal_release)
   664	        seal_branch = skill[seal_release:skill.index("report `seal-run:`", seal_release)]
   665	        for token in ("sealed-config-mismatch",
   666	                      "--taint-observed config --observed-by seal-run.py",
   667	                      "must not release"):
   668	            self.assertIn(token, seal_branch)

   670	    def test_ct_3_gate_child_detects_post_parent_config_swap(self):
   671	        fx = RunFixture(self)
   672	        self.assertEqual(fx.open().returncode, 0)
   673	        self.assertEqual(fx.plan_start_seal().returncode, 0)
   674	        self.assertEqual(fx.complete().returncode, 0)
   675	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
   676	            hook = textwrap.dedent(r'''
   677	                import builtins, json, os, sys
   678	                _builtins_open = builtins.open
   679	                _os_open = os.open
   680	                _target = os.environ["DOCAUDIT_COUNT_PATH"]
   681	                _log = os.environ["DOCAUDIT_COUNT_LOG"]
   682	                _done = os.environ["DOCAUDIT_SWAP_DONE"]
   683	                _swap = os.environ["DOCAUDIT_SWAP_BYTES"].encode("utf-8")
   684	                def _maybe_swap():
   685	                    if os.path.basename(sys.argv[0]) != "change-set-sha.py": return
   686	                    try:
   687	                        marker = _os_open(_done, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL, 0o600)
   688	                    except FileExistsError:
   689	                        return
   690	                    os.close(marker)
   691	                    replacement = _target + ".ct3-swap"
   692	                    fd = _os_open(replacement, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC, 0o600)
   693	                    try: os.write(fd, _swap)
   694	                    finally: os.close(fd)
   695	                    os.replace(replacement, _target)
   696	                def _same(path):
   697	                    try: return os.path.realpath(os.fspath(path)) == os.path.realpath(_target)
   698	                    except Exception: return False
   699	                def _record(kind, path):
   700	                    if not _same(path): return
   701	                    with _builtins_open(_log, "a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   702	                        handle.write(json.dumps({"kind":kind,"argv":sys.argv}) + "\n")
   703	                def counted_open(path, *args, **kwargs):
   704	                    handle = _builtins_open(path, *args, **kwargs)
   705	                    _record("builtins.open", path)
   706	                    return handle
   707	                def counted_os_open(path, *args, **kwargs):
   708	                    fd = _os_open(path, *args, **kwargs)
   709	                    _record("os.open", path)
   710	                    return fd
   711	                builtins.open = counted_open
   712	                os.open = counted_os_open
   713	                _maybe_swap()
   714	            ''')
   715	            write(os.path.join(temp, "sitecustomize.py"), hook)
   716	            log = os.path.join(temp, "opens.log")
   717	            write(log, "")
   718	            changed = dict(fx.config, ct3Race=True)
   719	            env = dict(
   720	                os.environ, PYTHONPATH=temp, DOCAUDIT_COUNT_PATH=fx.config_path,
   721	                DOCAUDIT_COUNT_LOG=log,
   722	                DOCAUDIT_SWAP_DONE=os.path.join(temp, "swap.done"),
   723	                DOCAUDIT_SWAP_BYTES=json.dumps(changed, sort_keys=True) + "\n")
   724	            proc = subprocess.run(
   725	                [sys.executable, script("decide-verdict.py"),
   726	                 "--run-dir", fx.run_dir, "--repo-root", fx.repo,
   727	                 "--config", fx.config_path, "--anchor-path", fx.anchor_rel,
   728	                 "--runid", fx.runid, "--expect-json", json.dumps(fx.evidence),
   729	                 "--date", "2026-08-18"],
   730	                capture_output=True, text=True, env=env)
   731	            self.assertEqual(proc.returncode, 3, proc.stdout + proc.stderr)
   732	            self.assertEqual(json.loads(proc.stdout)["reason"], "config-changed")
   733	            with open(fx.last_run, encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   734	                last_run = json.load(handle)
   735	            self.assertTrue(last_run["configAcceptanceRequired"])
   736	            self.assertEqual(last_run["expectedConfigSha"], fx.evidence["config"])
   737	            self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(os.path.join(fx.run_base, "lock")))
   738	            with open(log, encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   739	                events = [json.loads(line) for line in handle]
   740	            self.assertTrue(any(os.path.basename(item["argv"][0]) == "change-set-sha.py"
   741	                                for item in events))

   743	    def test_ct_3b_taint_nonownership_is_non_mutating(self):
   744	        fx = RunFixture(self)
   745	        self.assertEqual(fx.open().returncode, 0)
   746	        lock = os.path.join(fx.run_base, "lock")
   747	        with open(lock, "rb") as handle:
   748	            before = handle.read()
   749	        history_existed = os.path.exists(fx.history)
   750	        history_before = None
   751	        if history_existed:
   752	            with open(fx.history, "rb") as handle:
   753	                history_before = handle.read()
   754	        cases = [
   755	            ("20260818T120009Z-abcdef99", fx.run_dir, fx.evidence),
   756	            (fx.runid, fx.run_dir + "-wrong", fx.evidence),
   757	            (fx.runid, fx.run_dir, dict(fx.evidence, lockIno=fx.evidence["lockIno"] + 1)),
   758	        ]
   759	        for runid, run_dir, evidence in cases:
   760	            with self.subTest(runid=runid, run_dir=run_dir):
   761	                proc = fx.call(
   762	                    "decide-verdict.py", "--run-dir", run_dir, "--repo-root", fx.repo,
   763	                    "--runid", runid, "--expect-json", json.dumps(evidence),
   764	                    "--taint-observed", "config", "--observed-by", "mdq-index.sh")
   765	                self.assertEqual(proc.returncode, 3)
   766	                with open(lock, "rb") as handle:
   767	                    self.assertEqual(handle.read(), before)
   768	                self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(fx.last_run))
   769	                self.assertEqual(os.path.exists(fx.history), history_existed)
   770	                if history_existed:
   771	                    with open(fx.history, "rb") as handle:
   772	                        self.assertEqual(handle.read(), history_before)
   773	
   774	        missing = RunFixture(self)
   775	        self.assertEqual(missing.open().returncode, 0)
   776	        missing_lock = os.path.join(missing.run_base, "lock")
   777	        os.unlink(missing_lock)
   778	        proc = missing.call(
   779	            "decide-verdict.py", "--run-dir", missing.run_dir,
   780	            "--repo-root", missing.repo, "--runid", missing.runid,
   781	            "--expect-json", json.dumps(missing.evidence),
   782	            "--taint-observed", "config", "--observed-by", "mdq-index.sh")
   783	        self.assertEqual(proc.returncode, 3)
   784	        self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(missing.last_run))
   785	        self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(missing.history))
   786	
   787	        held = RunFixture(self)
   788	        self.assertEqual(held.open().returncode, 0)
   789	        held_lock = os.path.join(held.run_base, "lock")
   790	        write(held.history, json.dumps({"entries": [], "phase4Runs": []}) + "\n")
   791	        with open(held_lock, "rb") as handle:
   792	            held_lock_before = handle.read()
   793	        with open(held.history, "rb") as handle:
   794	            held_history_before = handle.read()
   795	        locker = subprocess.Popen(
   796	            [sys.executable, "-c", textwrap.dedent('''
   797	                import fcntl, os, sys
   798	                fd = os.open(sys.argv[1], os.O_RDWR)
   799	                fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
   800	                print("ready", flush=True)
   801	                sys.stdin.readline()
   802	                os.close(fd)
   803	            '''), held_lock], stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
   804	            stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True)
   805	        self.addCleanup(lambda: locker.poll() is None and locker.kill())
   806	        self.assertEqual(locker.stdout.readline().strip(), "ready")
   807	        try:
   808	            proc = held.call(
   809	                "decide-verdict.py", "--run-dir", held.run_dir,
   810	                "--repo-root", held.repo, "--runid", held.runid,
   811	                "--expect-json", json.dumps(held.evidence),
   812	                "--taint-observed", "config", "--observed-by", "mdq-index.sh")
   813	            self.assertEqual(proc.returncode, 3, proc.stdout + proc.stderr)
   814	            self.assertIn("lock is held by another process", proc.stdout)
   815	            with open(held_lock, "rb") as handle:
   816	                self.assertEqual(handle.read(), held_lock_before)
   817	            with open(held.history, "rb") as handle:
   818	                self.assertEqual(handle.read(), held_history_before)
   819	            self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(held.last_run))
   820	        finally:
   821	            locker.communicate("\n", timeout=5)

   872	    def test_ct_5b_complete_phase4_eligibility_table(self):
   873	        module = load_module("decide-verdict.py")
   874	        valid = {
   875	            ("full", "full", "completed"): True,
   876	            ("full", "full", "execution-failed"): False,
   877	            ("full", None, "skipped-full-run"): False,
   878	            ("full", None, "not-active"): False,
   879	            ("incremental", "diff", "completed"): False,
   880	            ("incremental", "diff", "execution-failed"): False,
   881	            ("incremental", None, "ref-invalid"): False,
   882	            ("incremental", None, "not-active"): False,
   883	        }
   884	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as repo:
   885	            for (mode, variant, state), eligible in valid.items():
   886	                with self.subTest(mode=mode, variant=variant, state=state):
   887	                    phase4 = {"findings": [], "codexReview": {
   888	                        "state": state, "promptVariant": variant,
   889	                        "carryForwardSha": "none"}}
   890	                    got, _codex, _normalized, _unresolved = module.validate_phase4_contract(
   891	                        repo, {"mode": mode}, phase4)
   892	                    self.assertEqual(got, eligible)
   893	            invalid = [
   894	                {"state": "completed", "carryForwardSha": "none"},
   895	                {"state": "completed", "promptVariant": "diff"},
   896	                {"state": "completed", "promptVariant": "full",
   897	                 "carryForwardSha": 1},
   898	                {"state": "future", "promptVariant": "diff", "carryForwardSha": "none"},
   899	                {"state": "completed", "promptVariant": None, "carryForwardSha": "none"},
   900	                {"state": "not-active", "promptVariant": "full", "carryForwardSha": "none"},
   901	                {"state": "completed", "promptVariant": "full", "carryForwardSha": "none"},
   902	            ]
   903	            modes = ["full", "incremental", "full", "incremental", "incremental",
   904	                     "full", "incremental"]
   905	            for codex, mode in zip(invalid, modes):
   906	                with self.subTest(invalid=codex, mode=mode):
   907	                    with self.assertRaises(module.Refused):
   908	                        module.validate_phase4_contract(
   909	                            repo, {"mode": mode}, {"findings": [], "codexReview": codex})

   921	    def test_ct_5_gate_records_and_measures_four_key_flips(self):
   922	        fx = RunFixture(self, config_extra={"codexReview": {"required": True}})
   923	
   924	        def full_phase(file_name, carry_sha="none"):
   925	            return {
   926	                "findings": [{"source": "codex-review", "file": file_name,
   927	                              "severity": "HIGH", "title": "sample"}],
   928	                "codexReview": {"state": "completed", "promptVariant": "full",
   929	                                "carryForwardSha": carry_sha},
   930	            }
   931	
   932	        self.assertEqual(fx.open().returncode, 0)
   933	        self.assertEqual(fx.plan_start_seal(mode="full", contract="0.16.0").returncode, 0)
   934	        self.assertEqual(fx.complete(phase4=full_phase("./docs/a.md")).returncode, 0)
   935	        first = fx.gate()
   936	        self.assertEqual(first.returncode, 0, first.stdout + first.stderr)
   937	        self.assertEqual(json.loads(first.stdout)["counts"]["phase4FlipsUnchangedContent"], 0)
   938	        with open(fx.history, encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   939	            first_history = json.load(handle)
   940	        self.assertEqual(first_history["phase4Runs"][0]["findings"],
   941	                         [{"file": "docs/a.md", "severity": "HIGH"}])
   942	
   943	        self.assertEqual(fx.open(runid="20260818T120001Z-abcdef13").returncode, 0)
   944	        self.assertEqual(fx.plan_start_seal(mode="full", contract="0.16.0").returncode, 0)
   945	        self.assertEqual(fx.complete(phase4=full_phase("docs/b.md:10")).returncode, 0)
   946	        second = fx.gate()
   947	        self.assertEqual(second.returncode, 0, second.stdout + second.stderr)
   948	        second_result = json.loads(second.stdout)
   949	        self.assertEqual(second_result["counts"]["phase4FlipsUnchangedContent"], 2)
   950	        self.assertTrue(any("Phase-4 instability: 2 file(s)" in warning
   951	                            for warning in second_result["warnings"]))
   952	        with open(fx.history, encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   953	            second_history = json.load(handle)
   954	        self.assertEqual(len(second_history["phase4Runs"]), 2)
   955	
   956	        self.assertEqual(fx.open(runid="20260818T120002Z-abcdef14").returncode, 0)
   957	        self.assertEqual(fx.plan_start_seal(mode="full", contract="0.16.0").returncode, 0)
   958	        different_carry = "sha256:" + "2" * 64
   959	        self.assertEqual(
   960	            fx.complete(phase4=full_phase("docs/a.md", different_carry)).returncode, 0)
   961	        third = fx.gate()
   962	        self.assertEqual(third.returncode, 0, third.stdout + third.stderr)
   963	        self.assertEqual(json.loads(third.stdout)["counts"]["phase4FlipsUnchangedContent"], 0)

   973	        fx = RunFixture(self, config_extra={"codexReview": {"required": True}})
   974	        self.assertEqual(fx.open().returncode, 0)
   975	        history = {
   976	            "entries": [],

   978	                "runid": "20260817T120000Z-abcdef11", "ts": "2026-08-17T12:00:00Z",

   980	                "contractVersion": "0.16.0", "configSha": fx.evidence["config"],
   981	                "carryForwardSha": "none", "unresolvedFileCount": 0,
   982	                "truncated": False,
   983	                "findings": [{"file": "docs/a.md", "severity": "HIGH"}],
   984	            }],
   985	        }
   986	        write(fx.history, json.dumps(history, sort_keys=True) + "\n")
   987	        args = [
   988	            "--mode", "full", "--repo-root", fx.repo,
   989	            "--config", fx.config_path, "--expect-config-sha", fx.evidence["config"],
   990	            "--available", "true", "--baseline-ok", "true",
   991	            "--history", fx.history, "--expect-history-sha", BAD_SHA,
   992	            "--worktree-digest", "sha256:" + "2" * 64,
   993	        ]
   994	        mismatch = run("codex-review-plan.py", *args, cwd=fx.repo)
   995	        self.assertEqual(mismatch.returncode, 7, mismatch.stdout + mismatch.stderr)
   996	        self.assertIn("sealed-history-mismatch", mismatch.stderr)
   997	        tainted = fx.call(
   998	            "decide-verdict.py", "--run-dir", fx.run_dir, "--repo-root", fx.repo,
   999	            "--runid", fx.runid, "--expect-json", json.dumps(fx.evidence),
  1000	            "--taint-observed", "history", "--observed-by", "codex-review-plan.py")
  1001	        self.assertEqual(tainted.returncode, 3, tainted.stdout + tainted.stderr)
  1002	        self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(fx.history))
  1003	        state_dir = os.path.dirname(fx.history)
  1004	        self.assertEqual(len([name for name in os.listdir(state_dir)
  1005	                              if name.startswith("docaudit-history.json.tainted-")]), 1)
  1006	        self.assertEqual(
  1007	            fx.open(runid="20260818T120001Z-abcdef13").returncode, 0)
  1008	        cold_args = list(args)
  1009	        cold_args[cold_args.index(BAD_SHA)] = "none"
  1010	        cold = run("codex-review-plan.py", *cold_args, cwd=ROOT)
  1011	        self.assertEqual(cold.returncode, 0, cold.stdout + cold.stderr)
  1012	        cold_result = json.loads(cold.stdout)
  1013	        self.assertIsNone(cold_result["carryForward"])
  1014	        self.assertEqual(cold_result["carryForwardSha"], "none")
